        local button=create("TextButton",{Name="AngelHubSprintButton",Text="SPRINT",Size=UDim2.fromOffset(72,48),
            Position=UDim2.new(1,-158,1,-96),TextSize=14,TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
            BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(25,25,25),BackgroundTransparency=.3,ZIndex=31},screenGui)
        create("UICorner",{CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)},button)
        button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if not stopped and (input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) then speed.touch[input]=true end
        end)
        button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)speed.touch[input]=nil;stopSprintIfReleased() end)
    end
    connectInput(userInputService.InputBegan,function(input,processed)
        if stopped or processed or AngelHub.listening or userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
            or input.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
        local name=input.KeyCode and input.KeyCode.Name
        if not name then return end
        if V["Enable Fly"] and name==V["Fly Up Key Value"] then fly.up[input.KeyCode]=true end
        if V["Enable Fly"] and name==V["Fly Down Key Value"] then fly.down[input.KeyCode]=true end
        if mm2 and name==V["Sprint Key Value"] then speed.sprintKey=name end
    end)
    connectInput(userInputService.InputEnded,function(input)
        if input.KeyCode then
            fly.up[input.KeyCode]=nil;fly.down[input.KeyCode]=nil
            if input.KeyCode.Name==speed.sprintKey then speed.sprintKey=nil;stopSprintIfReleased() end
        end
        fly.up[input]=nil;fly.down[input]=nil
        if speed.touch[input] then speed.touch[input]=nil;stopSprintIfReleased() end
    end)
    local function clearKeys()clearFlyInput();speed.sprintKey=nil;table.clear(speed.touch);stopSprintIfReleased() end
    connectInput(userInputService.WindowFocusReleased,clearKeys)
    connectInput(userInputService.TextBoxFocused,clearKeys)
    local function runMovement(step,name,dt)
        if not V[name] then return end
        local ok,err=pcall(step,dt)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub "..name..": "..tostring(err));AngelHub.setters[name](false) end
    end
    connectInput(run.Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if stopped then return end
        dt=math.clamp(tonumber(dt) or 0,0,.1)
        if dt<=0 then return end
        if V["Enable Speed"] or sprintHeld() then
            local ok,err=pcall(speedStep,dt)
            if not ok then
                warn("Angel Hub Speed/Sprint: "..tostring(err));speed.sprintKey=nil;table.clear(speed.touch)
                clearSprintTrail();AngelHub.setters["Enable Speed"](false)
            end
        end
        runMovement(flyStep,"Enable Fly",dt)
        runMovement(gravityStep,"Enable Gravity",dt)
    end)
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        stopped=true;clearKeys();removeButtons();removePlatform();releaseVelocity(fly);releaseVelocity(speed)
        clearSprintTrail();restoreSpeed();clearForce();restoreWorldGravity();AngelHub.resetGravity=nil
    end)
end


-- Standalone collision shield, backed by the Wurst MM2-independent module.
local function installAntiFlingFeatures(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local run=game:GetService("RunService")
    local connections, options = {}, {}
    local stopped=false
    local host={Players=playersService, LocalPlayer=localPlayer, workspace=workspace, featureConnections=connections}
    host.getCharacterParts=function()
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        return character, character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"), character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    host.TaskManager={Connect=function(_,callback)
        return run.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt) if not stopped then callback(dt) end end)
    end}
    host.disconnectFeatureConnection=function(key)
        local connection=connections[key]
        if connection then connection:Disconnect();connections[key]=nil end
    end
    host.createUniversalFeature=function(_,_,_,toggle)return {toggle=toggle} end
    host.addToggleOption=function(_,name,_,callback)options[name]=callback end
    host.addFeatureTooltip=function()end
    local framework={GetModule=function() return nil end}
    local activity={isActive=function(name)
        return (name=="fling" and AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy==true) or (name=="walkfling" and V["Walk Fling"]==true)
    end}
    local Shield=(function()
type Runtime = {
    framework: any,
    entity: any,
    host: any,
    services: any,
}

local Module = {
    Name = "AntiFling",
    PlaceId = 0,
    Events = {} :: {[string]: any},
    Initialized = false,
}

local activeCleanup: (() -> ())? = nil

function Module.init(context: Runtime): any
    local host: any = context.host
    local services: any = context.services
    local activity: any = services and services.activity
    local framework: any = context.framework
    local players: any = host.Players
    local localPlayer: any = host.LocalPlayer
    local world: any = host.workspace or workspace
    local featureConnections: any = host.featureConnections
    local disconnect: any = host.disconnectFeatureConnection

    local enabled: boolean = false
    local playerShield: boolean = true
    local impactShield: boolean = true
    local feature: any = nil
    local character: Model? = nil
    local links: {[BasePart]: {[BasePart]: any}} = {}
    local hazardSeen: {[BasePart]: number} = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}) :: any
    local nextScan: number = 0
    local dangerUntil: number = 0
    local suspended: boolean = false
    local warnedOverlap: boolean = false

    -- Limits are deliberately conservative. The shield is local physics, not
    -- server authority; do not change other people's CanCollide or global groups.
    local SCAN_INTERVAL: number = 0.15
    local RADIUS: number = 30
    local HAZARD_HOLD: number = 0.75
    local LINEAR_LIMIT: number = 80
    local SURFACE_LIMIT: number = 70
    local MAX_LINKS: number = 2048
    local MAX_PARTS: number = 200

    local function isBusy(): boolean
        return activity ~= nil and type(activity.isActive) == "function"
            and (activity.isActive("fling") or activity.isActive("walkfling"))
    end

    local function destroyLinks(): ()
        for _, perLocal: {[BasePart]: any} in pairs(links) do
            for _, constraint: any in pairs(perLocal) do
                pcall(function(): () constraint:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        table.clear(links)
        table.clear(hazardSeen)
        dangerUntil = 0
    end

    local function isToolPart(part: Instance): boolean
        local ok: boolean, result: any = pcall(function(): any
            return part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool")
        end)
        return ok and result ~= nil
    end

    local function listBodyParts(model: Model?, onlyCollidable: boolean): {BasePart}
        local parts: {BasePart} = {}
        if not model or not model.Parent then return parts end
        local ok: boolean, descendants: any = pcall(function(): any return model:GetDescendants() end)
        if not ok or type(descendants) ~= "table" then return parts end
        for _, object: Instance in ipairs(descendants) do
            if object:IsA("BasePart") and not isToolPart(object) then
                local part: BasePart = object :: BasePart
                if not onlyCollidable or part.CanCollide then table.insert(parts, part) end
            end
        end
        return parts
    end

    local function fastPart(part: BasePart, scale: number?): boolean
        local multiplier: number = scale or 1
        local radius: number = math.min(part.Size.Magnitude * 0.5, 20)
        return part.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > LINEAR_LIMIT * multiplier
            or part.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude * radius > SURFACE_LIMIT * multiplier
    end

    local function supportAssembly(root: BasePart, humanoid: Humanoid, owner: Model): (BasePart?, boolean)
        local ok: boolean, result: any = pcall(function(): any
            local params: RaycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
            params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {owner}
            params.IgnoreWater = true
            params.RespectCanCollide = true
            local distance: number = math.max(4, (tonumber(humanoid.HipHeight) or 2) + root.Size.Y * 0.5 + 2)
            return world:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, -distance, 0), params)
        end)
        if not ok then return nil, false end
        local ground: any = result and result.Instance
        if ground and ground:IsA("BasePart") then
            return ground.AssemblyRootPart or ground, true
        end
        return nil, true
    end

    local function synchronize(owner: Model, humanoid: Humanoid, root: BasePart, now: number): ()
        local ownParts: {BasePart} = listBodyParts(owner, true)
        local desired: {[BasePart]: boolean} = {}
        local candidates: {any} = {}
        local characterModels: {[Model]: boolean} = {}
        local roster: any = players:GetPlayers()

        -- Track every visible player body, not the shared world collision group.
        -- Nearest bodies get the bounded constraint budget first on large servers.
        for _, player: Player in ipairs(roster) do
            local other: Model? = player ~= localPlayer and player.Character or nil
            if other and other ~= owner and other.Parent then
                characterModels[other] = true
                local otherRoot: BasePart? = other:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") :: BasePart?
                local distance: number = otherRoot and (otherRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                table.insert(candidates, {model = other, distance = distance,
                    parts = if playerShield then listBodyParts(other, false) else {}})
            end
        end
        table.sort(candidates, function(a: any, b: any): boolean return a.distance < b.distance end)
        if playerShield then
            for _, candidate: any in ipairs(candidates) do
                for _, part: BasePart in ipairs(candidate.parts) do
                    desired[part] = true
                    if candidate.distance <= RADIUS and fastPart(part, nil) then
                        dangerUntil = math.max(dangerUntil, now + HAZARD_HOLD)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- The query is local and bounded. Anchored geometry, the supporting
        -- platform/assembly, seats and tools keep their normal interactions.
        -- A fast loose part is suppressed only briefly while it remains risky.
        if impactShield and not humanoid.Sit and not humanoid.SeatPart then
            local overlap: any = nil
            pcall(function(): ()
                overlap = OverlapParams.new()
                overlap.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                overlap.FilterDescendantsInstances = {owner}
                overlap.MaxParts = MAX_PARTS
                overlap.RespectCanCollide = true
            end)
            local ok: boolean, nearby: any = pcall(function(): any
                return world:GetPartBoundsInRadius(root.Position, RADIUS, overlap)
            end)
            if not ok or type(nearby) ~= "table" then
                if not warnedOverlap and type(host.notify) == "function" then
                    warnedOverlap = true
                    host.notify("Anti Fling: moving-object scan unavailable; player protection remains active.")
                end
            else
                local floorAssembly: BasePart?, floorChecked: boolean = supportAssembly(root, humanoid, owner)
                if not floorChecked then table.clear(hazardSeen) end
                for _, instance: Instance in ipairs(if floorChecked then nearby else {}) do
                    if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local part: BasePart = instance :: BasePart
                        local isCharacterPart: boolean = false
                        local ancestor: Instance? = part.Parent
                        while ancestor and ancestor ~= world do
                            if characterModels[ancestor :: any] then isCharacterPart = true break end
                            ancestor = ancestor.Parent
                        end
                        local inOwner: boolean = part:IsDescendantOf(owner)
                        local isTool: boolean = isToolPart(part)
                        local assembly: BasePart? = part.AssemblyRootPart or part
                        -- Ordinary weapon/tools remain interactive. An extreme
                        -- spinning/flying handle is treated as a hazard, at 3x
                        -- the loose-object threshold, only while it is nearby.
                        if part.Parent and part.CanCollide and not part.Anchored and not inOwner
                            and (not isCharacterPart or isTool)
                            and (not floorAssembly or assembly ~= floorAssembly)
                            and fastPart(part, if isTool then 3 else nil) then
                            hazardSeen[part] = now
                            dangerUntil = math.max(dangerUntil, now + HAZARD_HOLD)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            for part: BasePart, seen: number in pairs(hazardSeen) do
                if not part.Parent or now - seen > HAZARD_HOLD then
                    hazardSeen[part] = nil
                else
                    desired[part] = true
                end
            end
        else
            table.clear(hazardSeen)
        end

        -- Allocate the finite budget afresh in priority order: newly dangerous
        -- objects first, then nearest players. Otherwise 2,048 older/farther
        -- links could permanently starve a new incoming threat.
        local ordered: {BasePart} = {}
        local included: {[BasePart]: boolean} = {}
        local function include(part: BasePart): ()
            if desired[part] and part.Parent and not included[part] then
                included[part] = true
                table.insert(ordered, part)
            end
        end
        local hazards: {BasePart} = {}
        for part: BasePart in pairs(hazardSeen) do table.insert(hazards, part) end
        table.sort(hazards, function(a: BasePart, b: BasePart): boolean
            return (a.Position - root.Position).Magnitude < (b.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
        end)
        for _, part: BasePart in ipairs(hazards) do include(part) end
        if playerShield then
            for _, candidate: any in ipairs(candidates) do
                for _, part: BasePart in ipairs(candidate.parts) do include(part) end
            end
        end
        local accepted: {[BasePart]: {[BasePart]: boolean}} = {}
        local allocated: number = 0
        for _, other: BasePart in ipairs(ordered) do
            if allocated >= MAX_LINKS then break end
            local perLocal: {[BasePart]: boolean} = {}
            for _, own: BasePart in ipairs(ownParts) do
                if allocated >= MAX_LINKS then break end
                if own ~= other then
                    perLocal[own] = true
                    allocated += 1
                end
            end
            if next(perLocal) then accepted[other] = perLocal end
        end
        -- Remove old or lower-priority pairs before filling the new budget.
        for other: BasePart, perLocal: {[BasePart]: any} in pairs(links) do
            local wanted: {[BasePart]: boolean}? = accepted[other]
            for own: BasePart, constraint: any in pairs(perLocal) do
                if not wanted or not wanted[own] or not own.Parent or not other.Parent then
                    pcall(function(): () constraint:Destroy() end)
                    perLocal[own] = nil
                end
            end
            if next(perLocal) == nil then links[other] = nil end
        end
        for other: BasePart, wanted: {[BasePart]: boolean} in pairs(accepted) do
            local perLocal: {[BasePart]: any} = links[other] or {}
            links[other] = perLocal
            for own: BasePart in pairs(wanted) do
                if not perLocal[own] then
                    local ok: boolean, constraint: any = pcall(function(): any
                        local link: NoCollisionConstraint = Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")
                        link.Part0, link.Part1 = own, other
                        link.Parent = own
                        return link
                    end)
                    if ok and constraint then perLocal[own] = constraint end
                end
            end
            if next(perLocal) == nil then links[other] = nil end
        end
    end

    local function step(): ()
        if not enabled then return end
        local owner: Model?, humanoid: Humanoid?, root: BasePart? = host.getCharacterParts()
        if owner ~= character then
            destroyLinks()
            character = owner
            nextScan = 0
        end
        if not owner or not humanoid or not root or not owner.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            destroyLinks()
            return
        end
        if isBusy() then
            if not suspended then destroyLinks() end
            suspended = true
            return
        end
        if suspended then suspended = false nextScan = 0 end
        local now: number = os.clock()
        if now >= nextScan then
            nextScan = now + SCAN_INTERVAL
            local ok: boolean, err: any = pcall(synchronize, owner, humanoid, root, now)
            if not ok then
                -- A streaming object may disappear during the scan. Retry on
                -- the next interval, without leaving partial constraints stuck.
                destroyLinks()
                if type(host.warn) == "function" then host.warn("Anti Fling: " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
        end
        if now > dangerUntil or root.Anchored or humanoid.Sit or humanoid.SeatPart then return end
        -- Emergency damping is only armed by a nearby extreme mover. Normal
        -- sprinting, jumps, vehicles and game launch pads are not capped merely
        -- because this feature is on.
        local walkSpeed: number = math.max(16, tonumber(humanoid.WalkSpeed) or 16)
        local speed: any = framework and framework:GetModule("Speed Hack")
        if speed and speed.Enabled and speed.Options.Speed then
            walkSpeed = math.max(walkSpeed, tonumber(speed.Options.Speed.Value) or 0)
        end
        local jumpPower: number = math.max(50, tonumber(humanoid.JumpPower) or 50)
        local velocity: Vector3 = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local planar: Vector3 = Vector3.new(velocity.X, 0, velocity.Z)
        local limit: number = math.max(120, walkSpeed * 4)
        local riseLimit: number = math.max(180, jumpPower * 4)
        local nextVelocity: Vector3 = velocity
        local changed: boolean = false
        if planar.Magnitude > limit then
            local move: Vector3 = humanoid.MoveDirection
            local direction: Vector3 = move.Magnitude > 0.05 and move.Unit * walkSpeed or Vector3.zero
            nextVelocity = Vector3.new(direction.X, nextVelocity.Y, direction.Z)
            changed = true
        end
        if nextVelocity.Y > riseLimit then
            nextVelocity = Vector3.new(nextVelocity.X, riseLimit, nextVelocity.Z)
            changed = true
        end
        pcall(function(): ()
            if root.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude > 25 then
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            if changed then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = nextVelocity end
        end)
    end

    local function toggleAntiFling(value: boolean): ()
        enabled = value == true
        disconnect("AntiFling")
        destroyLinks()
        character = nil
        nextScan = 0
        suspended = false
        if enabled then
            featureConnections.AntiFling = host.TaskManager:Connect(step, nil, "Anti Fling")
        end
    end

    feature = host.createUniversalFeature(
        "Anti Fling",
        "Collision Shield: local player and extreme-object protection",
        2,
        toggleAntiFling,
        {categoryName = "Utilities"}
    )
    host.addToggleOption(feature, "Player Shield", true, function(value: boolean): ()
        playerShield = value == true
        nextScan = 0
        if not playerShield and not impactShield then destroyLinks() end
    end, "Prevent local physical collisions with other character bodies without changing their collision settings.")
    host.addToggleOption(feature, "Impact Shield", true, function(value: boolean): ()
        impactShield = value == true
        nextScan = 0
        if not playerShield and not impactShield then destroyLinks() end
    end, "Temporarily avoid nearby extreme-speed loose objects; support platforms, seated vehicles and ordinary tools are left alone.")
    host.addFeatureTooltip(feature,
        "Collision Shield only changes local collision pairs. Extremely fast/streamed objects and server-owned physics can still launch you.")

    activeCleanup = function(): ()
        toggleAntiFling(false)
    end
    Module.Initialized = true
    return feature
end

function Module.destroy(): ()
    if activeCleanup then pcall(activeCleanup) end
    activeCleanup = nil
    Module.Initialized = false
end

return Module

    end)()
    local feature=Shield.init({host=host, framework=framework, services={activity=activity}})
    AngelHub.suspendAntiFling=function()
        if not stopped and V["Anti Fling"] then feature.toggle(false);feature.toggle(true) end
    end
    for _,name in ipairs({"Player Shield","Impact Shield"}) do
        local old=AngelHub.setters[name]
        if old then AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value) old(value);options[name](V[name]==true) end end
        options[name](V[name]==true)
    end
    local old=AngelHub.setters["Anti Fling"]
    AngelHub.setters["Anti Fling"]=function(value)
        local on=not stopped and value==true
        old(on);feature.toggle(on)
    end
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;Shield.destroy();AngelHub.suspendAntiFling=nil end)
end

-- Shared player collection and projection for ESP, Nametags and HP.
-- GUI primitives replace executor-only Drawing so the same path works on mobile.
local function installPlayerVisuals(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local run=game:GetService("RunService")
    local stopped=false
    local pool={}
    local layer=create("Frame",{Name="AngelPlayerVisuals",Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundTransparency=1,Visible=true,ZIndex=3},screenGui)
    local selfText=create("TextLabel",{Name="AngelSelfHP",AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(.5,0),Size=UDim2.fromOffset(140,26),
        BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,Text="",Visible=false,Font=Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
        TextStrokeTransparency=0,TextSize=18,ZIndex=5},layer)
    local dark=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    local gray=Color3.fromRGB(120,120,120)
    local white=Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)
    local low,mid,high=Color3.fromRGB(255,70,70),Color3.fromRGB(255,220,60),Color3.fromRGB(85,255,85)
    local r6={{"Head","Torso"},{"Torso","Left Arm"},{"Torso","Right Arm"},{"Torso","Left Leg"},{"Torso","Right Leg"}}
    local r15={{"Head","UpperTorso"},{"UpperTorso","LowerTorso"},{"UpperTorso","LeftUpperArm"},{"LeftUpperArm","LeftLowerArm"},{"LeftLowerArm","LeftHand"},
        {"UpperTorso","RightUpperArm"},{"RightUpperArm","RightLowerArm"},{"RightLowerArm","RightHand"},
        {"LowerTorso","LeftUpperLeg"},{"LeftUpperLeg","LeftLowerLeg"},{"LeftLowerLeg","LeftFoot"},
        {"LowerTorso","RightUpperLeg"},{"RightUpperLeg","RightLowerLeg"},{"RightLowerLeg","RightFoot"}}
    local function rect(parent,name,transparency,z)
        return create("Frame",{Name=name,BackgroundTransparency=transparency or 0,BorderSizePixel=0,Visible=false,ZIndex=z or 4},parent)
    end
    local function label(parent,name,z)
        return create("TextLabel",{Name=name,BackgroundTransparency=1,BorderSizePixel=0,Text="",Visible=false,
            Font=Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,TextStrokeTransparency=0,TextColor3=white,ZIndex=z or 5},parent)
    end
    local function item()
        local frame=create("Frame",{Name="PlayerVisual",Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1),BackgroundTransparency=1,Visible=false},layer)
        return {frame=frame}
    end
    local function ensureEsp(e)
        if e.edges then return end
        local frame=e.frame;e.edges={};e.shadows={};e.bones={}
        e.fill=rect(frame,"Fill",.88,3)
        for i=1,8 do e.shadows[i]=rect(frame,"BoxOutline"..i,0,3);e.edges[i]=rect(frame,"Box"..i,0,4) end
        for i=1,14 do e.bones[i]=rect(frame,"Bone"..i,0,4) end
        e.tracerShadow=rect(frame,"TracerOutline",0,3);e.tracer=rect(frame,"Tracer",0,4)
    end
    local function ensureTag(e)
        if e.name then return end
        e.name=label(e.frame,"Name");e.detail=label(e.frame,"Details")
    end
    local function ensureHP(e)
        if e.hp then return end
        e.hpBack=rect(e.frame,"HPOutline",0,3);e.hp=rect(e.frame,"HPBar",0,4);e.hpText=label(e.frame,"HPText")
    end
    local function hideAll(list)for _,object in ipairs(list)do object.Visible=false end end
    local function hideEsp(e)
        if not e.edges then return end
        e.fill.Visible=false;e.tracer.Visible=false;e.tracerShadow.Visible=false
        hideAll(e.edges);hideAll(e.shadows);hideAll(e.bones)
    end
    local function hideTag(e)if e.name then e.name.Visible=false;e.detail.Visible=false end end
    local function hideHP(e)if e.hp then e.hp.Visible=false;e.hpBack.Visible=false;e.hpText.Visible=false end end
    local function remove(player)
        local entry=pool[player];pool[player]=nil
        if entry then entry.frame:Destroy() end
    end
    local function clear() for player in pairs(pool)do remove(player)end end
    local function line(obj,x1,y1,x2,y2,thickness,color)
        local dx,dy=x2-x1,y2-y1
        local length=math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)
        obj.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(.5,.5);obj.Position=UDim2.fromOffset((x1+x2)*.5,(y1+y2)*.5)
        obj.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(length,thickness);obj.Rotation=math.deg(math.atan2(dy,dx))
        obj.BackgroundColor3=color;obj.Visible=length>.1
    end
    local function finite(n)return type(n)=="number" and n==n and math.abs(n)<1e7 end
    local function project(camera,position)
        local v=camera:WorldToViewportPoint(position)
        if v and finite(v.X) and finite(v.Y) and finite(v.Z) and v.Z>.1 then return v end
    end
    local function boxOf(camera,root,humanoid,viewport)
        local half=root.Size.Y*.5
        local isR6=humanoid.RigType==Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6
        local top=project(camera,root.Position+Vector3.new(0,(isR6 and 2 or 2.6)*half,0))
        local bottom=project(camera,root.Position-Vector3.new(0,isR6 and 3*half or (humanoid.HipHeight+half),0))
        if not top or not bottom then return end
        local height=math.floor(bottom.Y-top.Y+.5)
        if height<0 or height>4000 then return end
        height=math.max(4,height)
        local width=math.floor(height*.55+.5);local x=math.floor((top.X+bottom.X)*.5+.5)
        local y=math.floor(top.Y+.5)
        if x+width*.5<0 or x-width*.5>viewport.X or y+height<0 or y>viewport.Y then return end
        return {x=x-width*.5,y=y,w=width,h=height,cx=x}
    end
    local visibleCache=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
    local function visible(player,character,root,camera)
        local now=os.clock();local cached=visibleCache[player]
        if cached and cached.character==character and now-cached.at<.1 then return cached.visible end
        local ok,result=pcall(function()
            local origin=camera.CFrame.Position
            local params=RaycastParams.new();params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude;params.IgnoreWater=true
            local excluded={};if localPlayer.Character then table.insert(excluded,localPlayer.Character)end
            for _=1,8 do
                params.FilterDescendantsInstances=excluded
                local hit=workspace:Raycast(origin,root.Position-origin,params)
                if not hit then return true end
                local part=hit.Instance
                if part and part:IsDescendantOf(character) then return true end
                if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Transparency>=.95 and not part.CanCollide then
                    table.insert(excluded,part)
                else return false end
            end
            return false
        end)
        local yes=ok and result==true
        visibleCache[player]={character=character,at=now,visible=yes}
        return yes
    end
    local function getRole(player,human)
        if not isMM2 then return nil end
        if human and human.Health<=0 then return "Dead" end
        return AngelHub.getMM2Role and AngelHub.getMM2Role(player) or nil
    end
    local function colour(role)
        return (role and V["Role Colours"] and V[role.." Colour"]) or white
    end
    local function allowed(role)
        return not role or V[role.." ESP"]~=false
    end
    local function health(humanoid)
        local n=tonumber(humanoid.Health) or 0;local max=tonumber(humanoid.MaxHealth) or 100
        if not finite(n) then n=0 end;if not finite(max) or max<=0 then max=100 end
        return math.max(0,n),math.clamp(n/max,0,1)
    end
    local function healthColor(f)return f<.5 and low:Lerp(mid,f*2) or mid:Lerp(high,(f-.5)*2)end
    local function setRect(obj,x,y,w,h,color)
        obj.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(x,y);obj.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(w,h);obj.BackgroundColor3=color;obj.Visible=true
    end
    local function updateESP(e,b,character,human,camera,color,viewport)
        ensureEsp(e)
        local mode=V["ESP Boxes"] or "Corner";local thickness=math.clamp(tonumber(V["Box Thickness"]) or 1,1,5)
        e.fill.Visible=V["Filled Box"]==true and mode~="Off"
        if e.fill.Visible then setRect(e.fill,b.x,b.y,b.w,b.h,color)end
        local x,y,w,h=b.x,b.y,b.w,b.h;local cw,ch=w*.3,h*.2
        local segments=mode=="Full" and {{x,y,x+w,y},{x+w,y,x+w,y+h},{x+w,y+h,x,y+h},{x,y+h,x,y}}
            or {{x,y,x+cw,y},{x,y,x,y+ch},{x+w,y,x+w-cw,y},{x+w,y,x+w,y+ch},
                {x,y+h,x+cw,y+h},{x,y+h,x,y+h-ch},{x+w,y+h,x+w-cw,y+h},{x+w,y+h,x+w,y+h-ch}}
        for i=1,8 do
            local segment=mode~="Off" and segments[i] or nil
            if segment then
                line(e.shadows[i],segment[1],segment[2],segment[3],segment[4],thickness+2,dark)
                line(e.edges[i],segment[1],segment[2],segment[3],segment[4],thickness,color)
            else e.shadows[i].Visible=false;e.edges[i].Visible=false end
        end
        local bones=human.RigType==Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 and r6 or r15
        for i=1,14 do
            local pair=V["Skeleton"] and bones[i]
            local a=pair and character:FindFirstChild(pair[1]);local bpart=pair and character:FindFirstChild(pair[2])
            local p1=a and a:IsA("BasePart") and project(camera,a.Position)
            local p2=bpart and bpart:IsA("BasePart") and project(camera,bpart.Position)
            if p1 and p2 then line(e.bones[i],p1.X,p1.Y,p2.X,p2.Y,1,color)else e.bones[i].Visible=false end
        end
        local tracer=V["Tracers"] or "Off"
        if tracer~="Off" then
            local x0,y0=viewport.X*.5,viewport.Y*.5
            if tracer=="Bottom" then y0=viewport.Y
            elseif tracer=="Cursor" then
                local ok,mouse=pcall(function()return userInputService:GetMouseLocation()end)
                if ok and mouse then x0,y0=mouse.X,mouse.Y end
            end
            line(e.tracerShadow,x0,y0,b.cx,b.y+b.h,3,dark);line(e.tracer,x0,y0,b.cx,b.y+b.h,1,color)
        else e.tracer.Visible=false;e.tracerShadow.Visible=false end
    end
    local function updateTag(e,b,player,role,dist,color,viewport)
        ensureTag(e)
        local size=math.clamp(tonumber(V["Nametag Text Size"]) or 14,10,24)
        local name=V["Display Name"] and player.DisplayName or player.Name
        local detail={}
        if V["Account Name"] and name~=player.Name then table.insert(detail,"@"..player.Name)end
        if V["Show Role"] and role then table.insert(detail,role)end
        if V["Nametag Distance"] then table.insert(detail,math.floor(dist+.5).." studs")end
        local text=table.concat(detail,"  ·  ")
        local width=math.max(10,math.min(240,viewport.X-8));local height=size+(text~="" and size or 0)
        local cx=math.clamp(b.cx,width*.5,math.max(width*.5,viewport.X-width*.5))
        local y=math.clamp(b.y-height-4,2,math.max(2,viewport.Y-height))
        e.name.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(cx-width*.5,y);e.name.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(width,size+2)
        e.name.Text=name;e.name.TextSize=size;e.name.TextColor3=color;e.name.Visible=true
        e.detail.Visible=text~=""
        if text~="" then e.detail.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(cx-width*.5,y+size);e.detail.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(width,size)
            e.detail.Text=text;e.detail.TextSize=math.max(10,size-2);e.detail.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(190,190,190) end
    end
    local function updateHP(e,b,human,dt)
        ensureHP(e)
        local value,frac=health(human)
        if e.humanoid~=human then e.humanoid=human;e.fraction=frac end
        e.fraction+=(frac-e.fraction)*math.min(1,math.max(0,dt)*10)
        local fh=math.max(1,math.floor(b.h*e.fraction+.5));local c=healthColor(e.fraction)
        setRect(e.hpBack,b.x-7,b.y-1,5,b.h+2,dark);setRect(e.hp,b.x-6,b.y+b.h-fh,3,fh,c)
        e.hpText.Visible=V["HP Health Text"]==true
        if e.hpText.Visible then e.hpText.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(b.x-46,b.y+b.h-fh-6)
            e.hpText.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(36,14);e.hpText.Text=tostring(math.ceil(value));e.hpText.TextSize=11;e.hpText.TextColor3=c end
    end
    local function frame(dt)
        local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera;local any=V["Player ESP"] or V["Nametags"] or V["HP Players"]
        if not cam then for _,e in pairs(pool)do e.frame.Visible=false end;selfText.Visible=false;return end
        local vp=cam.ViewportSize
        if not vp or vp.X<1 or vp.Y<1 then return end
        local myChar=localPlayer.Character;local myRoot=myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local origin=cam.CFrame.Position
        local seen={}
        if any then for _,player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do
            if player~=localPlayer then
                local character=player.Character;local hum=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if character and character.Parent and hum and root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local role=getRole(player,hum);local alive=hum.Health>0
                    local dist=(root.Position-origin).Magnitude
                    local b=allowed(role) and (alive or role=="Dead") and boxOf(cam,root,hum,vp)
                    if b then
                        local showESP=V["Player ESP"]
                        local showTag=V["Nametags"] and (V["Nametag Through Walls"] or visible(player,character,root,cam))
                        local showHP=V["HP Players"] and alive and (V["HP Through Walls"] or visible(player,character,root,cam))
                        if showESP or showTag or showHP then
                            seen[player]=true;local e=pool[player] or item();pool[player]=e;e.frame.Visible=true
                            local c=colour(role)
                            if showESP then
                                local espColor=c
                                if V["ESP Visibility Check"] and not visible(player,character,root,cam) then espColor=c:Lerp(gray,.55)end
                                updateESP(e,b,character,hum,cam,espColor,vp)
                            else hideEsp(e)end
                            if showTag then updateTag(e,b,player,role,dist,c,vp)else hideTag(e) end
                            if showHP then updateHP(e,b,hum,dt)else hideHP(e) end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end end
        for player,e in pairs(pool)do if not seen[player]then e.frame.Visible=false end end
        local human=myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        selfText.Visible=V["Self HP"]==true and human~=nil and human.Health>0
        if selfText.Visible then
            local hp,fraction=health(human);selfText.Text=tostring(math.ceil(hp));selfText.TextColor3=healthColor(fraction)
            selfText.TextSize=math.floor(18*math.clamp(tonumber(V["Self HP Scale"]) or 1,.8,1.6))
            selfText.Position=V["Self HP Position"]=="Bottom" and UDim2.new(.5,0,1,-110) or UDim2.new(.5,6,.5,30)
        end
    end
    local elapsed,lastWarning=0,-math.huge
    local signal=run.RenderStepped or run.Heartbeat
    connectInput(signal,function(dt)
        if stopped or not (V["Player ESP"] or V["Nametags"] or V["HP Players"] or V["Self HP"]) then return end
        elapsed+=math.max(0,tonumber(dt) or 0)
        if elapsed<1/60 then return end;local delta=elapsed;elapsed=0
        local ok,err=pcall(frame,delta)
        if not ok then
            for _,e in pairs(pool)do e.frame.Visible=false end;selfText.Visible=false
            local now=os.clock();if now-lastWarning>=5 then lastWarning=now;warn("Angel Hub player visuals: "..tostring(err))end
        end
    end)
    if playersService.PlayerRemoving then connectInput(playersService.PlayerRemoving,function(p)remove(p);visibleCache[p]=nil end)end
    for _,name in ipairs({"Player ESP","Nametags","HP Players","Self HP"})do
        local old=AngelHub.setters[name]
        if old then AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value)
            old(not stopped and value==true)
            if isMM2 and V[name] and AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles() end
            if not (V["Player ESP"] or V["Nametags"] or V["HP Players"]) then clear()
            else
                for _,e in pairs(pool)do
                    if not V["Player ESP"] then hideEsp(e)end
                    if not V["Nametags"] then hideTag(e) end
                    if not V["HP Players"] then hideHP(e) end
                end
            end
            if not V["Self HP"] then selfText.Visible=false end
            if not V[name] and AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles() end
        end end
    end
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;clear();layer:Destroy()end)
end

local function installChams(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local mm2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local run=game:GetService("RunService")
    local highlights={}
    local stopped=false
    local elapsed=0
    local function remove(player)
        local item=highlights[player];highlights[player]=nil
        if item then item:Destroy() end
    end
    local function clear()for player in pairs(highlights)do remove(player)end end
    local function update()
        local seen={}
        for _,player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do
            local character=player.Character
            local human=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if player~=localPlayer and character and character.Parent and human then
                local role=mm2 and ((human.Health<=0 and "Dead") or (AngelHub.getMM2Role and AngelHub.getMM2Role(player))) or nil
                local accepted=not role or V[role.." ESP"]~=false
                if accepted and (human.Health>0 or role=="Dead") then
                    seen[player]=true
                    local h=highlights[player]
                    if not h then h=Instance.new("Highlight");h.Name="AngelHubChams";h.Parent=screenGui;highlights[player]=h end
                    local color=(role and V["Role Colours"] and V[role.." Colour"]) or Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)
                    h.Adornee=character;h.FillColor=color;h.OutlineColor=color
                    h.FillTransparency=math.clamp(tonumber(V["Chams Fill Transparency"]) or .5,0,1)
                    h.OutlineTransparency=math.clamp(tonumber(V["Chams Outline Transparency"]) or 0,0,1)
                    h.DepthMode=V["Chams Through Walls"] and Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
                end
            end
        end
        for player in pairs(highlights)do if not seen[player]then remove(player)end end
    end
    local old=AngelHub.setters["Chams"]
    AngelHub.setters["Chams"]=function(value)
        local on=not stopped and value==true;old(on);elapsed=.2
        if on then if mm2 and AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles() end
        else clear();if AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles()end end
    end
    connectInput(run.Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if stopped or not V["Chams"] then return end
        elapsed+=math.max(tonumber(dt) or 0,0);if elapsed<.2 then return end;elapsed=0
        local ok,err=pcall(update)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Chams: "..tostring(err));AngelHub.setters["Chams"](false)end
    end)
    if playersService.PlayerRemoving then connectInput(playersService.PlayerRemoving,remove)end
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;clear()end)
end

-- MM2 pickup visualisation, adapted from the previous independent menu.
local function installCoinESP(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local enabled={}
    local stopped=false
    local shooter={}
    function shooter.gameplay()
        local ok,rs=pcall(function()return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")end)
        local remotes=ok and rs and rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        return remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
    end
    local function mm2Map()
        local candidates, fallback = workspace:GetChildren(), nil
        for _, model in ipairs(candidates) do
            local name = string.lower(model.Name)
            if model:IsA("Model") and not string.find(name, "lobby", 1, true)
                and not model:FindFirstChild("VotePad1") and not model:FindFirstChild("VotePads")
                and not model:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons") then
                if model:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or model:FindFirstChild("CoinAreas")
                    or model:FindFirstChild("Coins") or model:FindFirstChild("GunDrop") then return model end
                if not fallback and model:FindFirstChild("Spawns") then fallback = model end
            end
        end
        return fallback
    end
    AngelHub.findMM2Map=mm2Map
    local coinEsp = {}
    do
        local markers, connections = {}, {}
        local elapsed, dirty, serverFamily, activeMap, serverMap = 2, true, nil, nil, nil
        local familyOrder = {
            {"Egg", {"egg"}}, {"BeachBall", {"beachball", "beach ball"}},
            {"SnowToken", {"snowtoken", "snow token", "snowball", "snow ball"}},
            {"Candy", {"candy", "candies", "candied"}}, {"Heart", {"heart"}},
        }
        local function clear()
            for visual, box in pairs(markers) do
                markers[visual] = nil; pcall(function() box:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        local function disconnect()
            for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do connection:Disconnect() end
            table.clear(connections)
        end
        local function workspaceFamily()
            local ok, name = pcall(function() return string.lower(tostring(workspace:GetAttribute("CurrentEvent") or "")) end)
            if ok then
                for _, pair in ipairs({{"halloween", "Candy"}, {"christmas", "SnowToken"}, {"summer", "BeachBall"}, {"easter", "Egg"}, {"valentine", "Heart"}}) do
                    if string.find(name, pair[1], 1, true) then return pair[2] end
                end
            end
            for _, pair in ipairs({{"isValentines", "Heart"}, {"isChristmas", "SnowToken"}, {"isChristmasEnding", "SnowToken"}, {"IsSummer", "BeachBall"}, {"IsEaster", "Egg"}}) do
                local success, active = pcall(function() return workspace:GetAttribute(pair[1]) end)
                if success and active == true then return pair[2] end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function familyOf(part, active)
            local names = {}
            for _, child in ipairs(part:GetDescendants()) do table.insert(names, child.Name) end
            local text = string.lower(table.concat(names, " "))
            for _, data in ipairs(familyOrder) do
                for _, phrase in ipairs(data[2]) do
                    if string.find(text, phrase, 1, true) then return data[1] end
                end
            end
            return active or "Coin"
        end
        local function usable(part, map)
            if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") or not part:IsDescendantOf(map)
                or part:GetAttribute("Collected") == true or part:GetAttribute("Delete") == true then return false end
            local ancestor = part.Parent
            while ancestor and ancestor ~= map do
                if ancestor:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return false end
                if ancestor:IsA("BasePart") and (ancestor:GetAttribute("Collected") == true or ancestor:GetAttribute("Delete") == true) then return false end
                ancestor = ancestor.Parent
            end
            return ancestor == map
        end
        function coinEsp.toggle(value)
            disconnect(); clear(); serverFamily = nil; activeMap, serverMap = nil, nil; elapsed, dirty = 2, true
            if not value then return end
            local gameplay = shooter.gameplay()
            local started = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("CoinsStarted")
            if started and started:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(connections, started.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(bags)
                    serverFamily, serverMap = nil, mm2Map()
                    if type(bags) == "table" then
                        for _, family in ipairs({"Coin", "Candy", "SnowToken", "BeachBall", "Egg", "Heart"}) do
                            if bags[family] ~= nil then serverFamily = family; break end
                        end
                    end
                    dirty = true
                end))
            end
            local ended = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundEndFade")
            if ended and ended:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(connections, ended.OnClientEvent:Connect(function() serverFamily, serverMap = nil, nil; dirty = true end))
            end
        end
        function coinEsp.step(dt)
            if not isMM2 or not enabled["Coin ESP"] then return end
            elapsed += math.max(0, dt)
            if not dirty and elapsed < 1 then return end
            elapsed, dirty = 0, false
            local map = mm2Map()
            if map ~= activeMap then
                clear(); activeMap = map
                if serverMap ~= map then serverFamily, serverMap = nil, nil end
            end
            if not map then clear(); return end
            local active = serverFamily or workspaceFamily()
            local wanted = V["Coin In Use"] or "Auto (Server)"
            local found = {}
            local function consider(object)
                if not object:IsA("BasePart") then return end
                local visual = object
                if object.Name == "Coin_Server" or object.Name == "ServerCoinPart" then
                    visual = object:FindFirstChild("CoinVisual")
                end
                if not usable(visual, map) or found[visual] then return end
                local family = familyOf(visual, active)
                if wanted ~= "All" and wanted ~= "Auto (Server)" and wanted ~= family then return end
                if wanted == "Auto (Server)" and active and active ~= family then return end
                found[visual] = true
                local box = markers[visual]
                if not box then
                    box = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
                    box.Name = "AngelHub_CoinMarker"; box.Adornee = visual; box.AlwaysOnTop = true; box.ZIndex = 5
                    box.Parent = screenGui; markers[visual] = box
                end
                box.Color3 = V["Coin Colour"]
                box.Transparency = math.clamp(tonumber(V["Coin Transparency"]) or 0, 0, 1)
                box.Size = visual.Size * 1.3
            end
            local ok, collection = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CollectionService") end)
            if ok and collection then
                for _, object in ipairs(collection:GetTagged("CoinVisual")) do consider(object) end
            end
            for _, object in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
                if object:IsA("BasePart") and (object.Name == "CoinVisual" or object.Name == "Coin_Server"
                    or object.Name == "ServerCoinPart" or object:GetAttribute("CoinID") ~= nil) then consider(object) end
            end
            for visual, box in pairs(markers) do
                if not found[visual] then markers[visual] = nil; pcall(function() box:Destroy() end) end
            end
        end
        function coinEsp.cleanup() coinEsp.toggle(false) end
    end
    local old=AngelHub.setters["Coin ESP"]
    AngelHub.setters["Coin ESP"]=function(value)
        local on=isMM2 and not stopped and value==true
        old(on);enabled["Coin ESP"]=on;coinEsp.toggle(on)
    end
    connectInput(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if not stopped and enabled["Coin ESP"] then
            local ok,err=pcall(coinEsp.step,math.max(0,tonumber(dt) or 0))
            if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Coin ESP: "..tostring(err));AngelHub.setters["Coin ESP"](false) end
        end
    end)
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;coinEsp.cleanup();AngelHub.findMM2Map=nil end)
end

-- Universal utilities are independent of MM2 and restore only values they own.
local function installBasicUtilities(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local run=game:GetService("RunService")
    local lighting=game:GetService("Lighting")
    local stopped=false
    local groups={}
    local noclip=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
    local noclipCharacter=nil
    local clockValue=nil
    local jumpKey,jumpTouch,lastJump=nil,nil,-math.huge
    local function restore(name)
        local group=groups[name];groups[name]=nil
        if not group then return end
        for property,entry in pairs(group.fields)do pcall(function()
            if group.object[property]==entry.applied then group.object[property]=entry.previous end
        end)end
    end
    local function apply(name,object,properties)
        local group=groups[name]
        if not object then restore(name);return end
        if group and group.object~=object then restore(name);group=nil end
        if not group then group={object=object,fields={}};groups[name]=group end
        for property,value in pairs(properties)do
            local entry=group.fields[property]
            if not entry then entry={previous=object[property]};group.fields[property]=entry
            elseif object[property]~=entry.applied then entry.previous=object[property] end
            object[property]=value;entry.applied=value
        end
    end
    local function releaseNoclip()
        for part,entry in pairs(noclip)do
            pcall(function()if part.CanCollide==entry.applied then part.CanCollide=entry.previous end end)
            noclip[part]=nil
        end
        noclipCharacter=nil
    end
    local function noClipStep(character)
        if character~=noclipCharacter then releaseNoclip();noclipCharacter=character end
        if not character then return end
        for _,part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants())do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local record=noclip[part]
                if not record then record={previous=part.CanCollide};noclip[part]=record
                elseif part.CanCollide~=record.applied then record.previous=part.CanCollide end
                part.CanCollide=false;record.applied=false
            end
        end
        for part,record in pairs(noclip)do
            if not part:IsDescendantOf(character) then
                pcall(function()if part.CanCollide==record.applied then part.CanCollide=record.previous end end)
                noclip[part]=nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function update(dt)
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        local human=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if V["Noclip"] then noClipStep(character) elseif noclipCharacter then releaseNoclip() end
        if V["Custom Jump Power"] and human and human.Health>0 then
            apply("jump",human,{UseJumpPower=true,JumpPower=tonumber(V["Jump Power"]) or 50})
            if V["Jump Auto Jump"] and human.MoveDirection.Magnitude>.01 and human.FloorMaterial~=Enum.Material.Air then human.Jump=true end
        else restore("jump") end
        if V["Fullbright"] then apply("bright",lighting,{Brightness=tonumber(V["Fullbright Brightness"]) or 1})else restore("bright")end
        if V["Time Changer"] then
            if V["Fast Time"] then
                clockValue=((clockValue or lighting.ClockTime)+math.min(dt,.25)*6)%24
                apply("clock",lighting,{ClockTime=clockValue})
            else clockValue=nil;apply("clock",lighting,{ClockTime=tonumber(V["Time Value"]) or 14})end
        else clockValue=nil;restore("clock")end
        if V["Custom FOV"] then apply("fov",workspace.CurrentCamera,{FieldOfView=tonumber(V["FOV Value"]) or 70})else restore("fov")end
        if V["Custom Zoom"] then
            local max=tonumber(V["Zoom Max Distance"]) or 400;local min=math.min(tonumber(V["Zoom Min Distance"]) or .5,max)
            apply("zoom",localPlayer,{CameraMinZoomDistance=min,CameraMaxZoomDistance=max})
            if V["Unlock Forced First Person"] then apply("cameraMode",localPlayer,{CameraMode=Enum.CameraMode.Classic})else restore("cameraMode")end
        else restore("zoom");restore("cameraMode")end
    end
    local releases={["Noclip"]=releaseNoclip,["Custom Jump Power"]=function()restore("jump")end,
        ["Fullbright"]=function()restore("bright")end,["Time Changer"]=function()clockValue=nil;restore("clock")end,
        ["Custom FOV"]=function()restore("fov")end,["Custom Zoom"]=function()restore("zoom");restore("cameraMode")end}
    for name,release in pairs(releases)do
        local old=AngelHub.setters[name]
        if old then AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value)
            local on=not stopped and value==true;old(on)
            if not on then release() end
        end end
    end
    local oldJump=AngelHub.setters["Infinite Jump"]
    AngelHub.setters["Infinite Jump"]=function(value)
        oldJump(not stopped and value==true);jumpKey=nil;jumpTouch=nil;lastJump=-math.huge
    end
    local function jumpParts()
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        local human=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not human or human.Health<=0 or human.Sit or human.SeatPart or not root or root.Anchored then return end
        return human,root
    end
    local function jumpButtonTouch(input)
        local gui=localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        local touchGui=gui and gui:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
        local button=touchGui and touchGui:FindFirstChild("JumpButton",true)
        if not button or not button.Visible or not input.Position then return false end
        local p,at,size=input.Position,button.AbsolutePosition,button.AbsoluteSize
        return p.X>=at.X and p.Y>=at.Y and p.X<=at.X+size.X and p.Y<=at.Y+size.Y
    end
    connectInput(userInputService.InputBegan,function(input,processed)
        if stopped or not V["Infinite Jump"] or AngelHub.listening or userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        if not processed and (input.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.Space or input.KeyCode==Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA) then jumpKey=input.KeyCode
        elseif input.UserInputType==Enum.UserInputType.Touch and jumpButtonTouch(input) then jumpTouch=input end
    end)
    connectInput(userInputService.InputEnded,function(input)
        if jumpKey and input.KeyCode==jumpKey then jumpKey=nil end
        if jumpTouch==input then jumpTouch=nil end
    end)
    local function clearJump()jumpKey=nil;jumpTouch=nil end
    connectInput(userInputService.WindowFocusReleased,clearJump)
    connectInput(userInputService.TextBoxFocused,clearJump)
    connectInput(userInputService.JumpRequest,function()
        if stopped or not V["Infinite Jump"] or V["Infinite Jump Mode"]~="Normal" or V["Enable Fly"]
            or userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        local human,root=jumpParts()
        if not human or human.FloorMaterial~=Enum.Material.Air then return end
        local now=os.clock();local interval=math.clamp(tonumber(V["Infinite Jump Interval"]) or .1,.05,.6)
        if now-lastJump<interval then return end;lastJump=now
        local vel=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity;root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(vel.X,tonumber(V["Infinite Jump Power"]) or 50,vel.Z)
    end)
    local lastWarning=-math.huge
    connectInput(run.Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if stopped then return end
        if V["Infinite Jump"] and V["Infinite Jump Mode"]=="Rise" and not V["Enable Fly"] and (jumpKey or jumpTouch) then
            local human,root=jumpParts()
            if human and root then local vel=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity;root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(vel.X,tonumber(V["Infinite Jump Power"]) or 50,vel.Z) end
        end
        if not (V["Noclip"] or V["Custom Jump Power"] or V["Fullbright"] or V["Time Changer"] or V["Custom FOV"] or V["Custom Zoom"]) then return end
        local ok,err=pcall(update,math.clamp(tonumber(dt) or 0,0,.25))
        if not ok then local now=os.clock();if now-lastWarning>=5 then lastWarning=now;warn("Angel Hub utilities: "..tostring(err))end end
    end)
    if localPlayer.Idled then connectInput(localPlayer.Idled,function()
        if stopped or not V["Anti AFK"] then return end
        pcall(function()
            local virtual=game:GetService("VirtualUser");local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
            local viewport=camera and camera.ViewportSize
            local p=viewport and Vector2.new(viewport.X*.5,viewport.Y*.5) or Vector2.new(400,300)
            virtual:CaptureController();virtual:Button2Down(p,camera and camera.CFrame);virtual:Button2Up(p,camera and camera.CFrame)
        end)
    end)end
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        stopped=true;clearJump();releaseNoclip()
        for name in pairs(groups)do restore(name)end
    end)
end

-- MM2 voting: only active during intermission and explicitly enabled.
local function installAutoVote(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local enabled={}
    local stopped=false
    local mm2VoteStep
    local function conflict()
        return V["Enable Fly"] or V["Enable Speed"] or V["Auto Farm"] or V["Auto Win"]
            or V["Walk Fling"] or V["Auto Fling Selected Target"] or V["Air Walk"] or V["Air Swim"]
    end
    local function characterParts()
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        return character,character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"),character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    local function characterTeleport(destination)
        local character,human,root=characterParts()
        if not human or human.Health<=0 or not root or root.Anchored or not destination or not destination:IsA("BasePart")
            or not destination:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return false end
        return pcall(function()
            character:PivotTo(destination.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero;root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
    local vote = {}
    local voteElapsed, voteSignature, voteConnections = 0, nil, {}
    local voteRoundBlocked, previousBoardActive, lastVotePad = false, false, nil
    local voteState = {saved = {}}
    local function voteLobby()
        local function valid(candidate)
            if not candidate then return false end
            local ok, found = pcall(function()
                return candidate:FindFirstChild("VotePad1",true) or candidate:FindFirstChild("VotePads",true)
                    or candidate:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons",true)
            end)
            return ok and found ~= nil
        end
        for _, name in ipairs({"RegularLobby", "Lobby", "MainLobby", "HalloweenLobby"}) do
            local lobby = workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
            if valid(lobby) then return lobby end
        end
        for _, object in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do if valid(object) then return object end end
        local ok, tagged = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("VotePad") end)
        if ok and type(tagged) == "table" then
            for _, part in ipairs(tagged) do
                if part and part.Parent and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                    local parent = part.Parent
                    return parent.Name == "VoteIcons" and parent.Parent or parent
                end
            end
        end
        local nested = workspace:FindFirstChild("VotePads",true) or workspace:FindFirstChild("VotePad1",true) or workspace:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons",true)
        if nested and nested:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
            local parent = nested.Parent
            return parent and parent.Name == "VoteIcons" and parent.Parent or parent
        end
    end
    local function cleanVoteTitle(value)
        local text = string.match(tostring(value or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
        local upper = string.upper(text)
        if upper == "" or upper == "MAP NAME" or upper == "LOADING" or upper == "NONE"
            or upper == "MAP 1" or upper == "MAP 2" or upper == "MAP 3" then return nil end
        return text
    end
    local function voteTitle(label)
        if not label or not label:IsA("TextLabel") then return nil end
        return cleanVoteTitle(label.Text)
    end
    local function votingSlots(lobby)
        local slots, names, boardActive = {}, {}, false
        if not lobby then return slots, names, false end
        local pads = lobby:FindFirstChild("VotePads", true)
        local icons = lobby:FindFirstChild("VoteIcons", true)
        for slot = 1, 3 do
            local model = lobby:FindFirstChild("VotePad" .. slot, true)
            if model and model.Parent and model.Parent.Name == "VoteIcons" then model = nil end
            local icon = icons and icons:FindFirstChild("VotePad" .. slot)
            local label = model and model:FindFirstChild("MapName", true)
            local name = voteTitle(label) or voteTitle(icon and icon:FindFirstChild("MapName", true))
            if not name and icon then
                local map = icon:FindFirstChild("Map")
                if map and map:IsA("ObjectValue") and map.Value then name = cleanVoteTitle(map.Value.Name) end
            end
            local detector = pads and pads:FindFirstChild("Detector" .. slot, true)
            local pad = model and model:FindFirstChild("Pad", true)
            if pad and not pad:IsA("BasePart") then pad = nil end
            if detector and not detector:IsA("BasePart") then detector = nil end
            local surface = model and model:FindFirstChild("VoteInfoGui")
            local mapSurface = model and model:FindFirstChild("MapInfoGui")
            local iconSurface = icon and icon:FindFirstChild("Surface")
            local iconFlag = icon and icon:FindFirstChild("Voting")
            local mapInfo = icon and icon:FindFirstChild("MapInfo")
            local mapInfoSurface = mapInfo and mapInfo:FindFirstChild("Surface")
            if (surface and surface:IsA("SurfaceGui") and surface.Enabled)
                or (mapSurface and mapSurface:IsA("SurfaceGui") and mapSurface.Enabled)
                or (iconSurface and iconSurface:IsA("SurfaceGui") and iconSurface.Enabled)
                or (iconFlag and iconFlag:IsA("BoolValue") and iconFlag.Value)
                or (mapInfoSurface and mapInfoSurface:IsA("SurfaceGui") and mapInfoSurface.Enabled) then boardActive = true end
            if name then
                table.insert(names, name)
                local votes = icon and icon:FindFirstChild("Votes")
                local count = votes and votes:IsA("IntValue") and votes.Value or nil
                if count == nil and model then
                    local label = model:FindFirstChild("Votes",true)
                    if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then count = tonumber(string.match(label.Text,"%d+")) end
                end
                slots[slot] = {name = name, pad = pad, detector = detector, count = count}
            end
        end
        return slots, names, boardActive
    end
    local function voteGameplay()
        local ok, storage = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
        local remotes = ok and storage and storage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local gameplay = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
        return gameplay
    end
    local function restoreVotePads()
        for part, entry in pairs(voteState.saved) do
            pcall(function()
                if part.CFrame == entry.appliedFrame then part.CFrame = entry.frame end
                if part.CanCollide == entry.appliedCollide then part.CanCollide = entry.collide end
            end)
        end
        table.clear(voteState.saved)
    end
    function vote.reset(on)
        for _, connection in ipairs(voteConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(voteConnections)
        restoreVotePads()
        voteState.character, voteState.first, voteState.before, voteState.lastReset = nil,nil,nil,nil
        voteElapsed, lastVotePad, voteRoundBlocked, previousBoardActive = 1, nil, false, false
        if not on then return end
        local gameplay = voteGameplay()
        if not gameplay then return end
        for _, name in ipairs({"RoundStart", "RoleSelect", "ShowRoleSelect", "ShowRoleSelectNew", "CoinsStarted"}) do
            local remote = gameplay:FindFirstChild(name)
            if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(voteConnections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                    voteRoundBlocked, previousBoardActive, lastVotePad = true, true, nil
                    voteState.character, voteState.first = nil,nil
                    restoreVotePads()
                end))
            end
        end
        local roundEnd = gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundEndFade")
        if roundEnd and roundEnd:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            table.insert(voteConnections, roundEnd.OnClientEvent:Connect(function() voteRoundBlocked = false end))
        end
    end
    mm2VoteStep = function(dt, character, humanoid, root)
        if not isMM2 then return end
        voteElapsed += dt
        if voteElapsed < (enabled["Auto Vote"] and 0.25 or 1) then return end
        voteElapsed = 0
        local lobby = voteLobby()
        local slots, names, boardActive = votingSlots(lobby)
        local signature = table.concat(names, "\0")
        if signature ~= voteSignature then
            voteSignature = signature
            if AngelHub.cycleOptions and AngelHub.cycleOptions["Select Map"] then AngelHub.cycleOptions["Select Map"](names) end
        end
        if boardActive and not previousBoardActive then voteRoundBlocked = false
        elseif not boardActive and previousBoardActive then voteRoundBlocked = true end
        previousBoardActive = boardActive
        local timer = workspace:FindFirstChild("RoundTimerPart")
        local time = timer and timer:GetAttribute("Time")
        local selection, chosen = V["Select Map"], nil
        for _, data in pairs(slots) do if data.name == selection then chosen = data; break end end
        local pad = chosen and (chosen.pad or chosen.detector)
        local original = pad and voteState.saved[pad]
        local position = original and original.frame and original.frame.Position or (pad and pad.Position)
        local near = root and position and (root.Position-position).Magnitude <= 250
        -- Some servers do not replicate billboard Enabled. Only fall back when a
        -- non-positive round timer and actual map labels establish intermission.
        local active = boardActive or (type(time) == "number" and time <= 0 and #names > 0)
        if not enabled["Auto Vote"] or conflict() or voteRoundBlocked or not active or not character or not humanoid or not root
            or humanoid.Health<=0 or root.Anchored or not near or not pad or not pad:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
            or (type(time) == "number" and time > 0) then
            restoreVotePads(); lastVotePad = nil
            voteState.character, voteState.first, voteState.before, voteState.lastReset = nil,nil,nil,nil
            return
        end
        if voteState.character ~= character or lastVotePad ~= pad then
            restoreVotePads(); voteState.character, voteState.first = character,nil
            voteState.before, voteState.lastReset = chosen.count,nil
            lastVotePad = nil
        end
        if lastVotePad ~= pad or (root.Position-position).Magnitude > 6 then
            if not characterTeleport(pad) then return end
            lastVotePad = pad
        end
        local touched = false
        local firstPart = chosen.pad or chosen.detector
        for index, part in ipairs({firstPart, chosen.detector or firstPart}) do
            if part and (index == 1 or part ~= firstPart) then
                local entry = voteState.saved[part]
                if not entry then
                    entry = {frame=part.CFrame, collide=part.CanCollide}
                    voteState.saved[part] = entry
                end
                pcall(function()
                    part.CanCollide = false; entry.appliedCollide = false
                    part.CFrame = root.CFrame; entry.appliedFrame = root.CFrame
                end)
                if type(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
                    local ok = pcall(firetouchinterest,root,part,0)
                    pcall(firetouchinterest,root,part,1)
                    touched = touched or ok
                else touched = true end -- physical contact after teleport
            end
        end
        local now = os.clock()
        if touched and not voteState.first then voteState.first = now end
        local incremented = type(chosen.count) == "number" and type(voteState.before) == "number"
            and chosen.count > voteState.before
        if voteState.first and (incremented or now-voteState.first >= .22)
            and (not voteState.lastReset or now-voteState.lastReset >= .25)
            and enabled["Auto Vote"] and not voteRoundBlocked and localPlayer.Character == character
            and (type(time) ~= "number" or time <= 0) then
            restoreVotePads(); voteState.lastReset = now
            pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) end)
            pcall(function() humanoid.Health = 0 end)
            pcall(function() if humanoid.Health > 0 then character:BreakJoints() end end)
        end
    end
    local old=AngelHub.setters["Auto Vote"]
    AngelHub.setters["Auto Vote"]=function(value)
        local on=isMM2 and not stopped and value==true and not conflict()
        old(on);enabled["Auto Vote"]=on;vote.reset(on)
        if on then local character,human,root=characterParts();mm2VoteStep(1,character,human,root) end
    end
    connectInput(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if stopped or not isMM2 then return end
        local character,human,root=characterParts()
        local ok,err=pcall(mm2VoteStep,math.max(tonumber(dt) or 0,0),character,human,root)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Auto Vote: "..tostring(err));if enabled["Auto Vote"] then AngelHub.setters["Auto Vote"](false)end end
    end)
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;enabled["Auto Vote"]=false;vote.reset(false)end)
end

local function installItemESP(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local enabled={}
    local stopped=false
    local updateItemEsp,cleanupItemEsp
    local function characterParts()
        local char=localPlayer.Character
        return char,char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"),char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    local function findDroppedGun()return AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun and AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun()end
    local espGun, espTraps = nil, {}
    local espElapsed, trapGeneration = 0, 0
    local function markerPart(object)
        if object:IsA("BasePart") then return object end
        local handle = object:FindFirstChild("Handle", true)
        if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then return handle end
        return object:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end
    local function destroyMarker(marker)
        if not marker then return end
        pcall(function() marker.highlight:Destroy() end)
        pcall(function() marker.billboard:Destroy() end)
        for part, entry in pairs(marker.revealed) do
            pcall(function()
                if part.Transparency == entry.applied then part.Transparency = entry.original end
            end)
        end
        table.clear(marker.revealed)
    end
    local function createMarker(object, title)
        local part = markerPart(object)
        if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end
        local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "AngelHubItemOutline"
        highlight.Adornee = object
        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        highlight.FillTransparency = 1
        highlight.Parent = screenGui
        local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "AngelHubItemLabel"
        billboard.Adornee = part
        billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 30)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
        billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.Parent = screenGui
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        label.Parent = billboard
        return {object = object, part = part, title = title, highlight = highlight, billboard = billboard, label = label, revealed = {}}
    end
    local function revealTrap(marker, part)
        if not part:IsA("BasePart") or marker.revealed[part] then return end
        pcall(function()
            if part.Transparency >= 0.95 then
                marker.revealed[part] = {original = part.Transparency, applied = 0.35}
                part.Transparency = 0.35
            end
        end)
    end
    local function addTrap(object)
        if not enabled["Trap ESP"] or espTraps[object] then return end
        if (object.Name ~= "Trap" and object.Name ~= "TrapVisual") or not (object:IsA("BasePart") or object:IsA("Model")) then return end
        local parent = object.Parent
        while parent and parent ~= workspace do
            if espTraps[parent] then return end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
        local marker = createMarker(object, "Trap")
        if not marker then return end
        espTraps[object] = marker
        revealTrap(marker, object)
        for _, child in ipairs(object:GetDescendants()) do revealTrap(marker, child) end
    end
    local function clearTrapEsp()
        trapGeneration += 1
        for object, marker in pairs(espTraps) do destroyMarker(marker); espTraps[object] = nil end
    end
    local function startTrapEsp()
        clearTrapEsp()
        local generation = trapGeneration
        task.spawn(function()
            for i, object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if stopped or not enabled["Trap ESP"] or generation ~= trapGeneration then return end
                addTrap(object)
                if i % 200 == 0 then task.wait() end
            end
        end)
    end
    connectInput(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(object)
        local parent = object.Parent
        if enabled["Gun ESP"] and (object.Name == "GunDrop" or (parent and parent.Name == "GunDrop")) then
            espElapsed = 0.25
            local _, _, root = characterParts()
            local ok, err = pcall(updateItemEsp, 0, root)
            if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Item ESP error: " .. tostring(err)) end
        end
        if not enabled["Trap ESP"] then return end
        while parent and parent ~= workspace do
            if not espTraps[parent] and (parent.Name == "Trap" or parent.Name == "TrapVisual") then addTrap(parent) end
            local marker = espTraps[parent]
            if marker then revealTrap(marker, object); return end
            parent = parent.Parent
        end
        addTrap(object)
    end)
    local function refreshMarker(marker, root)
        local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera
        local origin=camera and camera.CFrame and camera.CFrame.Position or (root and root.Position)
        local distance=origin and (marker.part.Position-origin).Magnitude
        local visible=true
        local color = marker.title=="Gun" and Color3.fromRGB(70,160,255) or Color3.fromRGB(255,100,100)
        marker.highlight.OutlineColor = color
        marker.highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
        marker.highlight.Enabled = visible and V["Item Outline"] == true
        marker.billboard.Enabled = visible
        marker.billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
        marker.label.TextColor3 = color
        marker.label.TextSize = V["Item Text Size"]
        marker.label.Text = marker.title .. ((distance and V["Item Show Distance"]) and (" [" .. math.floor(distance + 0.5) .. "]") or "")
    end
    updateItemEsp = function(dt, root)
        if not isMM2 or (not enabled["Gun ESP"] and not enabled["Trap ESP"]) then return end
        espElapsed += dt
        if espElapsed < 0.25 then return end
        espElapsed = 0
        if enabled["Gun ESP"] then
            local gun = findDroppedGun()
            if espGun and (espGun.object ~= gun or not espGun.part:IsDescendantOf(workspace)) then
                destroyMarker(espGun); espGun = nil
            end
            if gun and not espGun then espGun = createMarker(gun, "Gun") end
            if espGun then refreshMarker(espGun, root) end
        end
        for object, marker in pairs(espTraps) do
            if not object.Parent or not object:IsDescendantOf(workspace) or not marker.part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                destroyMarker(marker); espTraps[object] = nil
            else
                refreshMarker(marker, root)
            end
        end
    end
    cleanupItemEsp = function()
        destroyMarker(espGun); espGun = nil
        clearTrapEsp()
    end
    for _,name in ipairs({"Gun ESP","Trap ESP"}) do
        local old=AngelHub.setters[name]
        AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value)
            local on=not stopped and isMM2 and value==true;old(on);enabled[name]=on
