            if name=="Trap ESP" then if on then startTrapEsp() else clearTrapEsp()end
            elseif not on then destroyMarker(espGun);espGun=nil end
            if on then local _,_,root=characterParts();espElapsed=.25;updateItemEsp(0,root)end
        end
    end
    connectInput(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if stopped or not isMM2 or not (enabled["Gun ESP"] or enabled["Trap ESP"]) then return end
        local _,_,root=characterParts()
        local ok,err=pcall(updateItemEsp,math.max(tonumber(dt) or 0,0),root)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Item ESP: "..tostring(err));AngelHub.setters["Gun ESP"](false);AngelHub.setters["Trap ESP"](false)end
    end)
    if V["Gun ESP"] then AngelHub.setters["Gun ESP"](true)end
    if V["Trap ESP"] then AngelHub.setters["Trap ESP"](true)end
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;cleanupItemEsp()end)
end

-- Button actions are explicit and never run merely because the menu loaded.
local function installBasicActions(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local stopped=false
    local function parts()
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        local human=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not human or human.Health<=0 or human.Sit or human.SeatPart or not root or root.Anchored then return end
        return character,human,root
    end
    local function motionConflict()
        return AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy or V["Enable Fly"] or V["Auto Farm"] or V["Auto Win"] or V["Auto Fling Selected Target"] or V["Walk Fling"] or V["Auto Vote"]
    end
    local function teleport(destination)
        local character,_,root=parts()
        if stopped or motionConflict() or not character or not destination or not destination:IsA("BasePart")
            or not destination:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return false end
        return pcall(function()
            character:PivotTo(destination.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0))
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero;root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
        end)
    end
    local function spawnPart(container)
        if not container then return end
        if container:IsA("BasePart") then return container end
        return container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation",true) or container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
    end
    AngelHub.actions["High Jump"]=function()
        if stopped or motionConflict() then return end
        local _,_,root=parts();if not root then return end
        local velocity=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(velocity.X,tonumber(V["High Jump Velocity"]) or 100,velocity.Z)
    end
    AngelHub.actions["Long Jump"]=function()
        if stopped or motionConflict() then return end
        local _,human,root=parts();if not root then return end
        local move=human.MoveDirection
        if move.Magnitude<.01 then
            local camera=workspace.CurrentCamera;local look=camera and camera.CFrame.LookVector
            if not look then return end;move=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)
        end
        if move.Magnitude<.01 then return end
        local target=move.Unit*(tonumber(V["Long Jump Speed"]) or 80)
        if V["Long Jump Mode"]=="Impulse" then
            local old=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            root:ApplyImpulse(Vector3.new(target.X-old.X,0,target.Z-old.Z)*root.AssemblyMass)
        elseif V["Long Jump Mode"]=="CFrame" then
            root.CFrame=root.CFrame+target*.15
        else
            local old=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity;root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(target.X,old.Y,target.Z)
        end
    end
    if isMM2 then
        AngelHub.actions["Teleport To Map"]=function()
            local map=AngelHub.findMM2Map and AngelHub.findMM2Map()
            local spawn=map and (map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or map:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true))
            teleport(spawnPart(spawn))
        end
        AngelHub.actions["Teleport To Lobby"]=function()
            local lobby=workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby")
            local spawn=lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true))
            teleport(spawnPart(spawn) or (lobby and lobby:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation",true)))
        end
        AngelHub.actions["Teleport To Player"]=function()
            local query=string.lower(string.match(tostring(V["Teleport Player Name"] or ""),"^%s*(.-)%s*$"))
            if query=="" then return end
            local found,exact,ambiguous
            for _,player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do if player~=localPlayer then
                local name,display=string.lower(player.Name),string.lower(player.DisplayName)
                if name==query then exact=player;break end
                if string.sub(name,1,#query)==query or string.sub(display,1,#query)==query then
                    if found then ambiguous=true else found=player end
                end
            end end
            local target=exact or (not ambiguous and found)
            local root=target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            teleport(root)
        end
        AngelHub.actions["Get Gun"]=function()
            if stopped or type(firetouchinterest)~="function" then return false end
            local _,_,root=parts();local drop=AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun and AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun()
            if not root or not drop or not drop:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return false end
            local touch=drop:IsA("BasePart") and drop or drop:FindFirstChild("Handle",true) or drop:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
            if not touch or not touch:IsA("BasePart") or not touch:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return false end
            local ok=pcall(firetouchinterest,root,touch,0)
            pcall(firetouchinterest,root,touch,1)
            return ok
        end
        connectInput(userInputService.InputBegan,function(input,processed)
            if stopped or processed or AngelHub.listening or userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
                or input.UserInputType~=Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
            if input.KeyCode and input.KeyCode.Name==V["Get Gun Key Value"] then AngelHub.actions["Get Gun"]() end
        end)
        local function canAutoGrab()
            if not AngelHub.getMM2Role or AngelHub.getMM2Role(localPlayer)~="Innocent"
                or not AngelHub.isMM2RoundActive or not AngelHub.isMM2RoundActive() then return false end
            local character,_,root=parts();if not character then return false end
            local backpack=localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            for _,holder in ipairs({character,backpack}) do
                if holder and (holder:FindFirstChild("Knife") or holder:FindFirstChild("Gun")) then return false end
            end
            local map=AngelHub.findMM2Map and AngelHub.findMM2Map()
            local spawns=map and (map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or map:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true))
            local mapRef=spawnPart(spawns)
            local lobby=workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("MainLobby")
            local lobbySpawns=lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true))
            local lobbyRef=spawnPart(lobbySpawns)
            if not mapRef or not lobbyRef then return false end
            return (root.Position-lobbyRef.Position).Magnitude>350 and (root.Position-mapRef.Position).Magnitude<=1500
        end
        local elapsed=0
        connectInput(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat,function(dt)
            if stopped or not V["Auto Get Gun"] then return end
            elapsed+=math.max(tonumber(dt) or 0,0)
            if elapsed>=.25 then elapsed=0;if canAutoGrab() then AngelHub.actions["Get Gun"]() end end
        end)
        local old=AngelHub.setters["Auto Get Gun"]
        AngelHub.setters["Auto Get Gun"]=function(value)
            old(not stopped and value==true);elapsed=.25
            if V["Auto Get Gun"] and AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles()
            elseif AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles() end
        end
    else
        local old=AngelHub.setters["Auto Get Gun"]
        if old then AngelHub.setters["Auto Get Gun"]=function()old(false)end end
    end
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true end)
end

local function installPlayerLists(screenGui)
    if game.PlaceId~=142823291 then return end
    local run=game:GetService("RunService")
    local stopped,signature,elapsed=false,nil,0
    local function refresh()
        if stopped then return end
        local names={}
        for _,player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do
            if player~=localPlayer and type(player.Name)=="string" and player.Name~="" then table.insert(names,player.Name)end
        end
        table.sort(names)
        local current=table.concat(names,"\0")
        if signature==current then return end
        signature=current
        for _,name in ipairs({"Kill Player","Fling Target"})do
            if AngelHub.listOptions[name] then AngelHub.listOptions[name](names)end
        end
    end
    if playersService.PlayerAdded then connectInput(playersService.PlayerAdded,refresh)end
    if playersService.PlayerRemoving then connectInput(playersService.PlayerRemoving,function()signature=nil;task.defer(refresh)end)end
    connectInput(run.Heartbeat,function(dt)elapsed+=math.max(0,dt);if elapsed>=1 then elapsed=0;refresh()end end)
    refresh()
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true end)
end

-- MM2-only SkidFling adaptation. Never started on injection.
local function installMM2Fling(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local run=game:GetService("RunService")
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local stopped=false
    local current,pendingRole,pendingAt,queue,nextAuto=nil,nil,0,{},0
    local roundEnded,bound,nextBind,roundConnections=false,nil,0,{}
    local function status(text)
        if AngelHub.setFlingStatus then AngelHub.setFlingStatus(text) end
    end
    local function characterParts()
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        local human=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not human or human.Health<=0 or not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        return character,human,root
    end
    local function spawnPart(container)
        if not container then return end
        if container:IsA("BasePart") then return container end
        return container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation",true) or container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
    end
    local function onMap(root,map)
        if not root or not map then return false end
        local lobby=workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("MainLobby")
        local lobbySpawn=lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true))
        local lobbyRef=spawnPart(lobbySpawn)
        local spawns=map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or map:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true)
        local container=map:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or map:FindFirstChild("CoinAreas") or map:FindFirstChild("Coins")
        local mapRef=spawnPart(spawns) or spawnPart(container) or map:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
        if not lobbyRef or not mapRef then return false end
        return (root.Position-lobbyRef.Position).Magnitude>350 and (root.Position-mapRef.Position).Magnitude<=1500
    end
    local function map()return AngelHub.findMM2Map and AngelHub.findMM2Map()end
    local function conflict(owner)
        return (V["Auto Farm"] and owner~="farm") or (V["Auto Win"] and owner~="win") or V["Walk Fling"] or V["Enable Fly"] or V["Enable Speed"]
            or V["Air Walk"] or V["Air Swim"] or V["Noclip"] or V["FreeCam"] or V["Auto Vote"]
    end
    local function clean()
        local state=current;current=nil
        AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy=false;AngelHub.mm2FlingOwner=nil
        if not state then return end
        if state.pin then pcall(function()state.pin:Destroy()end)end
        if state.human and state.seated~=nil then pcall(function()state.human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,state.seated)end)end
        if localPlayer.Character==state.character and state.root and state.root.Parent then pcall(function()
            state.root.Velocity=Vector3.zero;state.root.RotVelocity=Vector3.zero
            state.root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero;state.root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero
            local safeReturn=state.map==map()
            if not safeReturn and state.owner=="win" and AngelHub.winReturnSafe then
                safeReturn=AngelHub.winReturnSafe(state.character,state.home)==true
            end
            if not safeReturn and state.returnToLobby then
                local lobby=workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("MainLobby")
                local ref=spawnPart(lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true)))
                safeReturn=ref~=nil and (state.home.Position-ref.Position).Magnitude<=350
            end
            if state.human.Health>0 and safeReturn and state.home.Position.Y>state.destroyY+40 then
                state.character:PivotTo(state.home)
            end
        end)end
    end
    local function cancel()
        pendingRole=nil;AngelHub.mm2FlingNeedsRoles=false;table.clear(queue);clean();nextAuto=os.clock()+.7
        if AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles()end
    end
    local function bindRound()
        if os.clock()<nextBind then return end
        nextBind=os.clock()+1
        local ok,rs=pcall(function()return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")end)
        local remotes=ok and rs and rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local gameplay=remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
        if gameplay==bound then return end
        for _,connection in ipairs(roundConnections)do connection:Disconnect()end;table.clear(roundConnections)
        bound=gameplay;roundEnded=false
        if not gameplay then return end
        local ended=gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundEndFade")
        if ended and ended:IsA("RemoteEvent") then table.insert(roundConnections,ended.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()roundEnded=true;cancel()end))end
        for _,name in ipairs({"RoundStart","RoleSelect","ShowRoleSelect","ShowRoleSelectNew"})do
            local event=gameplay:FindFirstChild(name)
            if event and event:IsA("RemoteEvent") then table.insert(roundConnections,event.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(duration)
                if name=="RoundStart" and (not tonumber(duration) or tonumber(duration)<=0) then
                    roundEnded=true;cancel()
                else roundEnded=false end
            end))end
        end
    end
    local function selected()
        for _,player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do
            if player~=localPlayer and player.Name==V["Fling Target"] then return player end
        end
    end
    local function present(player)
        for _,candidate in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers()) do if candidate==player then return true end end
        return false
    end
    local function start(player,automatic,owner,stillValid,allowLobby,selectedAction)
        bindRound()
        local function reject(reason)
            if selectedAction then status(reason) end
            return false
        end
        if stopped or not isMM2 then return reject("Fling disponible solo en MM2") end
        if roundEnded and not selectedAction then return reject("Ronda terminada") end
        if conflict(owner) then return reject("Apaga Farm, Win, Fly, Speed, Noclip o Vote") end
        if current or pendingRole then return reject("Fling ya esta en curso") end
        if not player or player==localPlayer or not present(player) then return reject("Selecciona un jugador disponible") end
        if owner and (type(stillValid)~="function" or not stillValid()) then return false end
        local character,human,root=characterParts()
        local target=player.Character;local victim=target and target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local targetRoot=target and target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local currentMap=map()
        if not human or not root then return reject("Tu personaje no esta disponible") end
        if root.Anchored or human.Sit or human.SeatPart then return reject("Sal del asiento y libera el personaje") end
        if selectedAction and human.FloorMaterial==Enum.Material.Air then return reject("Espera a tocar suelo antes de Fling") end
        if not target or not target.Parent or not victim or victim.Health<=0 or victim.Sit or victim.SeatPart
            or not targetRoot or not targetRoot:IsA("BasePart") then return reject("Objetivo muerto, sentado o sin personaje") end
        if not selectedAction and ((not allowLobby and not onMap(root,currentMap)) or not onMap(targetRoot,currentMap)) then
            return reject("Objetivo fuera del mapa")
        end
        local returnToLobby=false
        if allowLobby and not onMap(root,currentMap) then
            local lobby=workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("MainLobby")
            local ref=spawnPart(lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true)))
            if not ref or (root.Position-ref.Position).Magnitude>350 then return false end
            returnToLobby=true
        end
        local destroyY=workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
        if type(destroyY)~="number" or destroyY~=destroyY or math.abs(destroyY)>1e6 then destroyY=-500 end
        if root.Position.Y<=destroyY+100 or targetRoot.Position.Y<=destroyY+100 then return reject("Fling detenido por proteccion del vacio") end
        if root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude>=50 then return reject("Espera a que tu personaje se estabilice") end
        local home;local ok=pcall(function()home=character:GetPivot()end)
        if not ok or not home then return reject("No se pudo guardar la posicion") end
        local pin
        local success,err=pcall(function()
            pin=Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            local okGuid,guid=pcall(function()return httpService:GenerateGUID(false)end)
            pin.Name=okGuid and guid or "AngelFlingVelocity"
            pin.Velocity=Vector3.new(1.8e9,1.8e9,1.8e9);pin.MaxForce=Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
            local seated;pcall(function()seated=human:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated)end)
            current={character=character,human=human,root=root,home=home,map=currentMap,player=player,
                target=target,targetRoot=targetRoot,victim=victim,pin=pin,seated=seated,destroyY=destroyY,
                start=os.clock(),phase=0,automatic=automatic,owner=owner,stillValid=stillValid,returnToLobby=returnToLobby,selectedAction=selectedAction,
                retries=owner and 0 or math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(V["Fling Retries"]) or 1),0,3)}
            AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy=true;AngelHub.mm2FlingOwner=owner
            if AngelHub.suspendAntiFling then AngelHub.suspendAntiFling()end
            pcall(function()human:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated,false)end)
            pin.Parent=root
        end)
        if not success then if pin then pcall(function()pin:Destroy()end)end;clean();warn("Angel Hub Fling: "..tostring(err));return reject("No se pudo iniciar Fling") end
        if selectedAction then status("Fling: "..player.Name) end
        return true
    end
    AngelHub.mm2StartOwnedFling=function(owner,player,stillValid,allowLobby)
        if (owner~="win" and owner~="farm") or pendingRole or #queue>0 then return false end
        return start(player,false,owner,stillValid,allowLobby==true)
    end
    AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling=function(owner)
        if current and current.owner==owner then clean();nextAuto=os.clock()+.7 end
    end
    local function role(roleName)
        if stopped or not isMM2 or current or pendingRole or #queue>0 or conflict() then return end
        bindRound();if roundEnded then return end
        pendingRole,pendingAt=roleName,os.clock();AngelHub.mm2FlingNeedsRoles=true
        if AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles()end
    end
    AngelHub.actions["Fling"]=function()
        if pendingRole or #queue>0 then status("Fling ya esta en curso");return end
        local ok,err=pcall(start,selected(),false,nil,nil,false,true)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Fling: "..tostring(err));cancel();status("Error al iniciar Fling")end
    end
    AngelHub.actions["Fling Murderer"]=function()role("Murderer")end
    AngelHub.actions["Fling Sheriff"]=function()role("Sheriff")end
    AngelHub.actions["Fling All Innocents"]=function()role("Innocent")end
    local offsets={{0,1.5,0},{0,-1.5,0},{2.25,1.5,-2.25},{-2.25,-1.5,2.25},{0,1.5,0},{0,-1.5,0}}
    local function step()
        bindRound()
        if pendingRole then
            for _,player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do
                local found=player~=localPlayer and AngelHub.getMM2Role and AngelHub.getMM2Role(player)
                if found==pendingRole or (pendingRole=="Sheriff" and found=="Hero")then table.insert(queue,player)end
            end
            if #queue>0 or os.clock()-pendingAt>=3 or conflict() then
                pendingRole=nil;AngelHub.mm2FlingNeedsRoles=false
                if AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles()end
            else return end
        end
        if current then
            local state=current;local root,targetRoot,victim=state.root,state.targetRoot,state.victim
            local now=os.clock()
            if (roundEnded and not state.selectedAction) or conflict(state.owner) or (state.owner and not state.stillValid()) or localPlayer.Character~=state.character or not root.Parent or state.human.Health<=0
                or not present(state.player) or state.player.Character~=state.target or not state.target.Parent or victim.Health<=0 or victim.Sit or victim.SeatPart
                or not targetRoot.Parent or state.map~=map() or (not state.selectedAction and not onMap(targetRoot,state.map))
                or root.Position.Y<=state.destroyY+40 or targetRoot.Position.Y<=state.destroyY+40
                or (state.automatic and (not V["Auto Fling Selected Target"] or selected()~=state.player)) then
                clean();nextAuto=now+.7;return
            end
            local velocity=targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if velocity.Magnitude>500 then clean();nextAuto=now+1;return end
            if now-state.start>=3 then
                if state.retries<=0 then clean();nextAuto=now+.7;return end
                state.retries-=1;state.start=now
            end
            state.phase+=1;local offset=offsets[(state.phase-1)%#offsets+1]
            local lead=Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z)*.15
            local frame=CFrame.new(targetRoot.Position+lead)*CFrame.new(offset[1],offset[2],offset[3])*CFrame.Angles(math.rad(state.phase*100),0,0)
            -- Pose via the model pivot, compensating for the root-to-pivot offset.
            local pivot=state.character:GetPivot()
            local rootToPivot=root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(pivot)
            root.CFrame=frame
            state.character:PivotTo(frame*rootToPivot)
            root.Velocity=Vector3.new(1.8e8,1.8e9,1.8e8)
            root.RotVelocity=Vector3.new(1.8e9,1.8e9,1.8e9)
            return
        end
        while #queue>0 do local target=table.remove(queue,1);if start(target,false) then return end end
        if V["Auto Fling Selected Target"] and os.clock()>=nextAuto and not conflict() then nextAuto=os.clock()+1;start(selected(),true,nil,nil,false,true)end
    end
    local old=AngelHub.setters["Auto Fling Selected Target"]
    AngelHub.setters["Auto Fling Selected Target"]=function(value)
        local on=isMM2 and not stopped and value==true and not conflict();old(on)
        if value==true and not on then status("Apaga modulos de movimiento o Farm/Win")
        elseif on and not selected() then status("Selecciona un jugador disponible")
        elseif not on then status("Auto Fling apagado") end
        if not on and (not current or not current.owner) then cancel()end
    end
    for _,name in ipairs({"Noclip","Enable Fly","Enable Speed","Auto Vote"})do
        local setter=AngelHub.setters[name]
        if setter then AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value)
            if value==true and (current or pendingRole or V["Auto Fling Selected Target"]) then
                AngelHub.setters["Auto Fling Selected Target"](false);cancel()
            end
            return setter(value)
        end end
    end
    connectInput(run.Heartbeat,function()
        if stopped or not isMM2 or not (current or pendingRole or #queue>0 or V["Auto Fling Selected Target"]) then return end
        local ok,err=pcall(step)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Fling: "..tostring(err));AngelHub.setters["Auto Fling Selected Target"](false);cancel();status("Error durante Fling")end
    end)
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        stopped=true;cancel();AngelHub.mm2StartOwnedFling=nil;AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling=nil
        for _,c in ipairs(roundConnections)do c:Disconnect()end;table.clear(roundConnections)
    end)
end

-- Auto Farm MM2, adapted from the previous menu. All movement is opt-in.
local function installMM2Farm(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local enabled=V
    local run=game:GetService("RunService")
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local stopped=false
    local extensionSteps={}
    local mm2FarmStep=function()end
    local groups={}
    local function message(text) if AngelHub.status then AngelHub.status(text) else warn("Angel Hub: "..tostring(text)) end end
    local function listen(signal,callback) return connectInput(signal,callback) end
    local function restore(name)
        local entry=groups[name];if not entry then return end
        for property,previous in pairs(entry.original)do pcall(function()
            if entry.object[property]==entry.applied[property] then entry.object[property]=previous end
        end)end
        groups[name]=nil
    end
    local function apply(name,object,properties)
        local group=groups[name]
        if not object then restore(name);return end
        if group and group.object~=object then restore(name);group=nil end
        if not group then group={object=object,original={},applied={}};groups[name]=group end
        for property,value in pairs(properties)do pcall(function()
            if group.original[property]==nil then group.original[property]=object[property]end
            object[property]=value;group.applied[property]=value
        end)end
    end
    local function characterParts()
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        local humanoid=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health<=0 or not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") then return character,nil,nil end
        return character,humanoid,root
    end
    local function mm2Map()return AngelHub.findMM2Map and AngelHub.findMM2Map()end
    local function spawnPart(container)
        if not container then return end
        if container:IsA("BasePart") then return container end
        return container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation",true) or container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
    end
    local function characterTeleport(destination)
        local character,human,root=characterParts()
        if not human or not root or root.Anchored or not destination or not destination:IsA("BasePart") or not destination:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return false end
        return pcall(function()character:PivotTo(destination.CFrame+Vector3.new(0,3,0));root.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.zero;root.AssemblyAngularVelocity=Vector3.zero end)
    end
    local farmConnections, farmMap, farmCharacter, farmRoundMap = {}, nil, nil, nil
    local farmWaitingMap, farmAmount, farmLimit, farmElapsed, farmTotal = nil, 0, 50, 0, 0
    local farmTickElapsed, farmScanElapsed, farmCoins = 0, 0, {}
    local farmDone, farmBagSampleKnown = false, false
    local farmGeneration,grabNext=0,0
    extensionSteps.farmFull = {resetDone=false}
    local farmBelowEngaged, farmHomeCFrame, farmHoverTarget = false, nil, nil
    local farmCollision = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local farmOnMap = function() return false end
    local leaveFarmPark = function() return true end
    local farmBlacklist = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local farmTarget, farmTargetAt, farmBestDistance
    local farmCounterSetters = {
        ["Coins Collected"] = AngelHub.setters["Coins Collected"],
        ["Farm Time"] = AngelHub.setters["Farm Time"],
    }
    local function farmCounter(name, value)
        local setter = farmCounterSetters[name]
        if setter then setter(value) end
    end
    if farmCounterSetters["Coins Collected"] then
        AngelHub.setters["Coins Collected"] = function(value)
            local amount = tonumber(value)
            if not amount or amount ~= amount or amount < 0 or amount > 1000000000 then return end
            farmTotal = math.floor(amount)
            farmCounter("Coins Collected", farmTotal)
        end
    end
    if farmCounterSetters["Farm Time"] then
        AngelHub.setters["Farm Time"] = function(value)
            local minutes, seconds
            if type(value) == "string" then minutes, seconds = value:match("^(%d+):(%d%d)$") end
            local elapsed = minutes and seconds and (tonumber(minutes) * 60 + tonumber(seconds)) or tonumber(value)
            if not elapsed or elapsed ~= elapsed or elapsed < 0 or elapsed > 1000000000 or (seconds and tonumber(seconds) >= 60) then return end
            farmElapsed = elapsed
            local whole = math.floor(elapsed)
            farmCounter("Farm Time", string.format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(whole / 60), whole % 60))
        end
    end
    local function farmStopMovement()
        local wasBelow, previousCharacter, home = farmBelowEngaged, farmCharacter, farmHomeCFrame
        farmBelowEngaged, farmHoverTarget, farmHomeCFrame = false, nil, nil
        restore("farmBody"); restore("farmSize")
        for part, original in pairs(farmCollision) do
            pcall(function() if part.CanCollide == false then part.CanCollide = original end end)
            farmCollision[part] = nil
        end
        if wasBelow and previousCharacter == localPlayer.Character and home then
            local _, humanoid, root = characterParts()
            if humanoid and root and not root.Anchored and farmOnMap(root, farmMap) and mm2Map() == farmMap then
                pcall(function()
                    previousCharacter:PivotTo(home)
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    local function farmStop()
        farmGeneration+=1
        for _, connection in ipairs(farmConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
        table.clear(farmConnections)
        farmStopMovement()
        leaveFarmPark()
        farmMap, farmCharacter, farmRoundMap, farmWaitingMap, farmTarget = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
        table.clear(farmCoins); table.clear(farmBlacklist)
    end
    local function farmLobbyRef()
        local lobby = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("MainLobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby")
        if not lobby then
            for _, object in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if string.find(string.lower(object.Name), "lobby", 1, true) then lobby = object; break end
            end
        end
        local spawns = lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns", true))
        return spawnPart(spawns) or (lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation", true)
            or lobby:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)))
    end
    farmOnMap = function(root, map)
        if not root or not map then return false end
        local lobbyRef = farmLobbyRef()
        local spawns = map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or map:FindFirstChild("Spawns", true)
        local container = map:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or map:FindFirstChild("CoinAreas") or map:FindFirstChild("Coins")
        local reference = spawnPart(spawns) or spawnPart(container)
            or map:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        -- Fail closed when either location is unknown; never move while in lobby.
        if not lobbyRef or not reference then return false end
        return (root.Position - lobbyRef.Position).Magnitude > 350
            and (root.Position - reference.Position).Magnitude <= 1500
    end
    local farmPark
    leaveFarmPark = function()
        local parked = farmPark
        if not parked then return true end
        local character, humanoid, root = characterParts()
        local nearPlatform = character == parked.character and humanoid and root
            and (root.Position - parked.position).Magnitude <= 120
        if nearPlatform then
            if root.Anchored or not parked.map or parked.map.Parent ~= workspace then
                message("Safe Zone: map return unavailable; platform kept")
                return false
            end
            local ok = pcall(function() character:PivotTo(parked.returnFrame) end)
            if not ok then message("Safe Zone: return failed; platform kept"); return false end
            pcall(function()
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        farmPark = nil
        pcall(function() parked.part:Destroy() end)
        return true
    end
    local function parkFarmCharacter(character, root, map, returnFrame)
        if not returnFrame or not map or map.Parent ~= workspace or not farmOnMap(root, map) then return false end
        if farmPark and not leaveFarmPark() then return false end
        local original = returnFrame.Position
        local destroyY = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
        if type(destroyY) ~= "number" or destroyY ~= destroyY or math.abs(destroyY) > 1000000 then destroyY = -500 end
        local safeY = math.max(original.Y + 280, 380, destroyY + 80)
        local platformPosition = Vector3.new(original.X + 650, safeY, original.Z + 650)
        local lobbyRef = farmLobbyRef()
        if lobbyRef and (platformPosition - lobbyRef.Position).Magnitude < 400 then
            platformPosition = Vector3.new(original.X - 650, safeY, original.Z - 650)
        end
        if lobbyRef and (platformPosition - lobbyRef.Position).Magnitude < 400 then return false end
        local part
        local ok = pcall(function()
            part = Instance.new("Part")
            part.Name = "AngelHub_FarmSafeZone"
            part.Anchored = true; part.CanCollide = true; part.CanQuery = false; part.CanTouch = false
            part.CastShadow = false; part.Size = Vector3.new(64, 4, 64)
            part.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; part.Transparency = 0.15
            part.CFrame = CFrame.new(platformPosition)
            part.Parent = workspace
        end)
        if not ok or not part or not part.Parent then
            if part then pcall(function() part:Destroy() end) end
            message("Safe Zone: could not create platform")
            return false
        end
        local moved = pcall(function() character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(platformPosition + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))) end)
        if not moved then pcall(function() part:Destroy() end); message("Safe Zone: move failed"); return false end
        farmPark = {character = character, map = map, part = part, returnFrame = returnFrame, position = platformPosition}
        pcall(function()
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end)
        return true
    end
    local function farmUsable(coin, map)
        if not coin or not coin.Parent or not coin:IsA("BasePart") or not coin:IsDescendantOf(map) then return false end
        if coin:GetAttribute("Collected") == true or coin:GetAttribute("Delete") == true then return false end
        local visual = coin:FindFirstChild("CoinVisual")
        if visual and (visual:GetAttribute("Collected") == true or visual:GetAttribute("Delete") == true) then return false end
        return true
    end
    local function farmScan(map)
        local found = {}
        local container = map:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer")
        local searchRoot = container or map
        for _, object in ipairs(searchRoot:GetDescendants()) do
            if (object.Name == "Coin_Server" or object.Name == "ServerCoinPart") and farmUsable(object, map) then
                table.insert(found, object)
            end
        end
        farmCoins = found
    end
    local function farmEvents()
        local ok, storage = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
        local remotes = ok and storage and storage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local gameplay = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
        local collected = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("CoinCollected")
        local ended = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundEndFade")
        if not collected or not collected:IsA("RemoteEvent") or not ended or not ended:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return nil end
        return gameplay, collected, ended
    end
    local function farmStart(gameplay, collected, ended)
        farmStop()
        farmAmount, farmLimit = 0, 50
        extensionSteps.farmFull.resetDone, extensionSteps.farmFull.resetAt = false,nil
        farmBagSampleKnown = false
        farmTickElapsed, farmScanElapsed, farmDone = 0, 0.5, false
        farmCounter("Coins Collected", farmTotal)
        local whole = math.floor(farmElapsed)
        farmCounter("Farm Time", string.format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(whole / 60), whole % 60))
        table.insert(farmConnections, collected.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(_, amount, limit)
            if not enabled["Auto Farm"] then return end
            local character, humanoid, root = characterParts()
            local map = mm2Map()
            if not humanoid or not farmOnMap(root, map) or (farmWaitingMap and farmWaitingMap == map) then return end
            if farmRoundMap ~= map then farmRoundMap, farmAmount, farmLimit, farmDone, farmBagSampleKnown = map, 0, 50, false, false end
            if farmMap ~= map or character ~= farmCharacter then
                farmStopMovement()
                farmMap, farmCharacter = map, character
                farmTarget, farmScanElapsed = nil, 0.5
                table.clear(farmCoins); table.clear(farmBlacklist)
            end
            if type(limit) == "number" and limit >= 50 then farmLimit = limit end
            if type(amount) == "number" and amount >= 0 then
                if amount > farmAmount then
                    farmTotal += farmBagSampleKnown and (amount - farmAmount) or 1
                    farmCounter("Coins Collected", farmTotal)
                    farmTarget, farmScanElapsed = nil, 0.5
                end
                if farmAmount>=math.max(50,farmLimit) and amount<math.max(50,farmLimit) then farmGeneration+=1 end
                farmAmount, farmBagSampleKnown = amount, true
                if amount < math.max(50, farmLimit) then
                    farmDone = false
                    extensionSteps.farmFull.resetDone, extensionSteps.farmFull.resetAt = false,nil
                end
            end
        end))
        table.insert(farmConnections, ended.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
            if not enabled["Auto Farm"] then return end
            farmWaitingMap = farmMap or mm2Map();farmGeneration+=1
            extensionSteps.farmFull.resetDone, extensionSteps.farmFull.resetAt = false,nil
            farmStopMovement(); leaveFarmPark(); farmMap, farmCharacter, farmRoundMap, farmTarget = nil, nil, nil, nil
            table.clear(farmCoins)
        end))
        for _, name in ipairs({"RoundStart", "RoleSelect", "ShowRoleSelect", "ShowRoleSelectNew", "CoinsStarted"}) do
            local remote = gameplay:FindFirstChild(name)
            if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(farmConnections, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(duration)
                    if name ~= "RoundStart" or (tonumber(duration) and tonumber(duration) > 0) then farmWaitingMap = nil end
                end))
            end
        end
    end
    AngelHub.farmFullTicket=function()return farmGeneration end
    AngelHub.farmFullAllowed=function()
        local character,human,root=characterParts();local map=mm2Map()
        return not stopped and enabled["Auto Farm"] and V["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"] and farmAmount>=math.max(50,farmLimit)
            and character~=nil and human~=nil and root~=nil and not root.Anchored and not human.Sit
            and farmOnMap(root,map) and not (farmWaitingMap and farmWaitingMap==map) and not AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy
    end
    mm2FarmStep = function(dt, character, humanoid, root)
        if not enabled["Auto Farm"] then return end
        farmElapsed += math.max(dt, 0)
        local seconds = math.floor(farmElapsed)
        farmCounter("Farm Time", string.format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(seconds / 60), seconds % 60))
        farmTickElapsed += dt
        if farmTickElapsed < 0.1 then return end
        local elapsed = farmTickElapsed
        farmTickElapsed = 0
        local map = mm2Map()
        if V["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"] and os.clock()>=grabNext and AngelHub.farmFullAllowed() then
            grabNext=os.clock()+.25
            if AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles()end
            local role=AngelHub.getMM2Role and AngelHub.getMM2Role(localPlayer)
            local backpack=localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            local gun=character:FindFirstChild("Gun") or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun"))
            local knife=character:FindFirstChild("Knife") or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife"))
            if gun and AngelHub.farmGrabShoot then AngelHub.farmGrabShoot()
            elseif role=="Innocent" and not knife and AngelHub.actions["Get Gun"] then AngelHub.actions["Get Gun"]()end
        end
        if farmPark then
            if V["Farm Safe Zone"] and character == farmPark.character and humanoid and root
                and farmPark.map == map and (root.Position - farmPark.position).Magnitude <= 120 then return end
            if not leaveFarmPark() then return end
        end
        if not character or not humanoid or not root or root.Anchored or not farmOnMap(root, map) or (farmWaitingMap and farmWaitingMap == map) then
            farmStopMovement(); farmMap, farmCharacter, farmTarget = nil, nil, nil
            return
        end
        if farmRoundMap ~= map then farmRoundMap, farmAmount, farmLimit, farmDone, farmBagSampleKnown = map, 0, 50, false, false end
        if map ~= farmMap or character ~= farmCharacter then
            farmStopMovement()
            farmMap, farmCharacter = map, character
            farmTarget, farmScanElapsed = nil, 0.5
            table.clear(farmCoins); table.clear(farmBlacklist)
        end
        if farmAmount >= math.max(50, farmLimit) then
            local wasBelow, belowHome = farmBelowEngaged, farmHomeCFrame
            farmStopMovement()
            if V["Reset When Full Coin Bag"] == true and not extensionSteps.farmFull.resetDone then
                local full = extensionSteps.farmFull
                if not full.resetAt then full.resetAt = os.clock() + (V["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"] == true and 2.2 or 0) end
                if os.clock() >= full.resetAt and enabled["Auto Farm"] and localPlayer.Character == character then
                    full.resetDone = true
                    pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) end)
                    pcall(function() humanoid.Health = 0 end)
                    pcall(function() if humanoid.Health > 0 then character:BreakJoints() end end)
                end
                return
            end
            if not farmDone then
                farmDone = true
                local spawns = map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or map:FindFirstChild("Spawns", true)
                local part = spawnPart(spawns)
                if V["Farm Safe Zone"] then
                    local returnFrame = belowHome or (part and (part.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)))
                    if not parkFarmCharacter(character, root, map, returnFrame) then message("Safe Zone unavailable") end
                elseif not wasBelow and part and farmOnMap(root, map) then
                    characterTeleport(part)
                end
            end
            return
        end
        farmScanElapsed += elapsed
        if farmScanElapsed >= 0.5 then farmScanElapsed = 0; farmScan(map) end
        local best, bestDistance
        local now = os.clock()
        for _, coin in ipairs(farmCoins) do
            if farmUsable(coin, map) and (not farmBlacklist[coin] or farmBlacklist[coin] <= now) then
                local distance = (coin.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if not bestDistance or distance < bestDistance then best, bestDistance = coin, distance end
            end
        end
        if not best then
            farmTarget = nil
            if farmBelowEngaged then
                local y = farmHoverTarget and farmHoverTarget.Y or root.Position.Y
                farmHoverTarget = Vector3.new(root.Position.X, y, root.Position.Z)
            else farmStopMovement() end
            return
        end
        if best ~= farmTarget then
            farmTarget, farmTargetAt, farmBestDistance = best, now, bestDistance
        elseif bestDistance < farmBestDistance - 0.35 then
            farmBestDistance, farmTargetAt = bestDistance, now
        end
        if now - farmTargetAt > 6 then
            farmBlacklist[best] = now + 6; farmTarget = nil
            if farmBelowEngaged then
                local y = farmHoverTarget and farmHoverTarget.Y or root.Position.Y
                farmHoverTarget = Vector3.new(root.Position.X, y, root.Position.Z)
            else farmStopMovement() end
            return
        end
        do -- Below is the only Auto Farm movement mode.
            if not farmBelowEngaged then
                if humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then return end
                farmHomeCFrame, farmBelowEngaged = root.CFrame, true
            end
            for _, object in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if object:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if farmCollision[object] == nil then farmCollision[object] = object.CanCollide end
                    object.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
            apply("farmBody", humanoid, {PlatformStand = true, AutoRotate = false})
            apply("farmSize", root, {Size = Vector3.new(4, 8, 4)})
            local destroyY = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
            if type(destroyY) ~= "number" or destroyY ~= destroyY or math.abs(destroyY) > 1000000 then destroyY = -500 end
            local low = math.max(destroyY + 40, farmHomeCFrame.Position.Y - 90)
            local high = math.max(low, farmHomeCFrame.Position.Y + 80)
            farmHoverTarget = Vector3.new(best.Position.X, math.clamp(best.Position.Y - 3, low, high), best.Position.Z)
            if bestDistance <= 5 and type(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
                pcall(firetouchinterest, root, best, 0); pcall(firetouchinterest, root, best, 1)
            end
            return
        end
    end
    local physicsOk, farmPhysicsSignal = pcall(function() return run.PreSimulation end)
    if not physicsOk or not farmPhysicsSignal then
        local ok, signal = pcall(function() return run.Stepped end)
        farmPhysicsSignal = ok and signal or nil
    end
    if farmPhysicsSignal then
        listen(farmPhysicsSignal, function()
            if stopped or not enabled["Auto Farm"] or not farmBelowEngaged or not farmHoverTarget then return end
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local character, humanoid, root = characterParts()
            if character ~= farmCharacter or not humanoid or not root or root.Anchored then return end
            if not farmOnMap(root, farmMap) then farmStopMovement(); return end
            local destroyY = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
            if type(destroyY) ~= "number" or destroyY ~= destroyY or math.abs(destroyY) > 1000000 then destroyY = -500 end
            if root.Position.Y <= math.max(destroyY + 40, farmHomeCFrame.Position.Y - 130) then
                AngelHub.setters["Auto Farm"](false); message("Auto Farm stopped: void guard"); return
            end
            local delta = farmHoverTarget - root.Position
            local flat = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
            local speed = math.clamp(V["Farm Speed"], 5, 60)
            local direction = flat.Magnitude > 0.35 and flat.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(direction.X * speed, math.clamp(delta.Y * 10, -speed, speed), direction.Z * speed)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end)
            if not ok then
                warn("Angel Hub Below physics error: " .. tostring(err))
                if enabled["Auto Farm"] then AngelHub.setters["Auto Farm"](false); message("Auto Farm stopped: physics error") end
            end
        end)
    end
    local auraCoins, auraScanElapsed, auraTickElapsed = {}, 0, 0
    local auraCurrent, auraState
    local function restoreCoinAura()
        local coin, saved = auraCurrent, auraState
        auraCurrent, auraState = nil, nil
        if not coin or not saved then return end
        pcall(function() if coin.CFrame == saved.appliedCFrame then coin.CFrame = saved.cframe end end)
        pcall(function() if coin.CanCollide == saved.appliedCollide then coin.CanCollide = saved.collide end end)
        if saved.visual then
            pcall(function() if saved.visual.Anchored == saved.appliedAnchored then saved.visual.Anchored = saved.anchored end end)
        end
        if saved.weld then
            pcall(function() if saved.weld.Enabled == saved.appliedWeld then saved.weld.Enabled = saved.weldEnabled end end)
        end
    end
    local function resetCoinAura()
        restoreCoinAura(); table.clear(auraCoins); auraScanElapsed, auraTickElapsed = 0.5, 0
    end
    local function mm2CoinAuraStep(dt, root)
        if not enabled["Coins Aura"] then return end
        auraTickElapsed += dt
        if auraTickElapsed < 0.08 then return end
        local elapsed = auraTickElapsed; auraTickElapsed = 0
        local map = mm2Map()
        if not root or root.Anchored or not farmOnMap(root, map) then restoreCoinAura(); return end
        auraScanElapsed += elapsed
        if auraScanElapsed >= 0.5 then
            auraScanElapsed = 0; table.clear(auraCoins)
            for _, object in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
                if (object.Name == "Coin_Server" or object.Name == "ServerCoinPart") and farmUsable(object, map) then
                    table.insert(auraCoins, object)
                end
            end
        end
        local best, nearest
        for _, coin in ipairs(auraCoins) do
            if farmUsable(coin, map) then
                local position = coin == auraCurrent and auraState.cframe.Position or coin.Position
                local distance = (position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if distance <= 15 and (not nearest or distance < nearest) then best, nearest = coin, distance end
            end
        end
        if best ~= auraCurrent then
            restoreCoinAura()
            if best then
                auraCurrent = best
                auraState = {cframe = best.CFrame, collide = best.CanCollide}
                local visual = best:FindFirstChild("CoinVisual")
                if visual and visual:IsA("BasePart") then
                    auraState.visual, auraState.anchored = visual, visual.Anchored
                    local weld = visual:FindFirstChildOfClass("WeldConstraint")
                    if weld then auraState.weld, auraState.weldEnabled = weld, weld.Enabled end
                end
            end
        end
        if not best then return end
        local saved = auraState
        if saved.appliedCFrame and best.CFrame ~= saved.appliedCFrame then saved.cframe = best.CFrame end
        if saved.appliedCollide ~= nil and best.CanCollide ~= saved.appliedCollide then saved.collide = best.CanCollide end
        if saved.visual then saved.visual.Anchored = true; saved.appliedAnchored = true end
        if saved.weld then saved.weld.Enabled = false; saved.appliedWeld = false end
        best.CanCollide = false; saved.appliedCollide = false
        best.CFrame = root.CFrame; saved.appliedCFrame = root.CFrame
        pcall(firetouchinterest, root, best, 0); pcall(firetouchinterest, root, best, 1)
    end

    local oldFarm=AngelHub.setters["Auto Farm"]
    local function conflict()
        return V["Auto Win"] or V["Enable Fly"] or V["Enable Speed"] or V["Noclip"] or V["Air Walk"] or V["Air Swim"]
            or V["FreeCam"] or V["Walk Fling"] or V["Auto Fling Selected Target"] or V["Auto Vote"] or AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy
    end
    AngelHub.setters["Auto Farm"]=function(value)
        local on=isMM2 and not stopped and value==true
        if on and V["Auto Farm"] then return end
        if on then
            if conflict() or not farmPhysicsSignal or (farmPark and not leaveFarmPark()) then
                oldFarm(false);message("Auto Farm: movimiento incompatible o retorno no disponible");return false
            end
            local gameplay,collected,ended=farmEvents()
            if not gameplay then oldFarm(false);message("Auto Farm: señales de MM2 no disponibles");return false end
            oldFarm(true);farmStart(gameplay,collected,ended)
            if AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles()end
        else oldFarm(false);farmStop();if AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles()end end
    end
    local oldGrab=AngelHub.setters["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"]
    AngelHub.setters["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"]=function(value)
        local before=V["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"]
        oldGrab(isMM2 and not stopped and value==true)
        if V["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"]~=before then farmGeneration+=1 end
        if V["Auto Farm"] and V["Auto Grab Gun And Shoot"] and AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles()
        elseif AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles()end
    end
    local oldSafe=AngelHub.setters["Farm Safe Zone"]
    AngelHub.setters["Farm Safe Zone"]=function(value)
        oldSafe(value==true)
        if not value then leaveFarmPark() elseif V["Auto Farm"] and farmAmount>=math.max(50,farmLimit) then farmDone=false end
    end
    for _,name in ipairs({"Noclip","Enable Fly","Enable Speed","Auto Vote","Auto Fling Selected Target","Auto Win"})do
        local setter=AngelHub.setters[name]
        if setter then AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value)
            if value==true and V["Auto Farm"] then AngelHub.setters["Auto Farm"](false)end
            if value==true and farmPark and not leaveFarmPark() then return false end
            return setter(value)
        end end
    end
    local oldAura=AngelHub.setters["Coins Aura"]
    AngelHub.setters["Coins Aura"]=function(value)
        local on=isMM2 and not stopped and value==true and type(firetouchinterest)=="function"
        oldAura(on);resetCoinAura()
    end
    connectInput(run.Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if stopped then return end
        if V["Coins Aura"] then
            local _,_,root=characterParts()
            if V["Auto Win"] or AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy then restoreCoinAura()
            else
                local auraOK,auraErr=pcall(mm2CoinAuraStep,dt,root)
                if not auraOK then warn("Angel Hub Coins Aura: "..tostring(auraErr));AngelHub.setters["Coins Aura"](false)end
            end
        end
        if not V["Auto Farm"] then return end
        if conflict() then AngelHub.setters["Auto Farm"](false);return end
        local character,human,root=characterParts()
        local ok,err=pcall(mm2FarmStep,dt,character,human,root)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Auto Farm: "..tostring(err));AngelHub.setters["Auto Farm"](false)end
    end)
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        stopped=true;oldFarm(false);oldAura(false);resetCoinAura();farmStop()
        AngelHub.farmFullTicket=nil;AngelHub.farmFullAllowed=nil
    end)
end

-- Auto Unbox uses the live MM2 catalogue and a verified balance; no Robux.
local function installMM2Unbox(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local enabled=V
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local stopped=false
    local mm2UnboxStep=function()end
    local function message(text) if AngelHub.status then AngelHub.status(text) else warn("Angel Hub: "..tostring(text)) end end
    local unbox = {}
    do
    -- Shop support uses only catalog and prices present in the live Sync module.
    local unboxCatalog, unboxList = {}, {}
    local unboxBusy, unboxCount, unboxNext, unboxReceipt, unboxRevealUntil = false, 0, 0, nil, 0
    local unboxRefreshAt, unboxRefreshing, unboxGeneration = 0, false, 0
    local pendingBoxSelection, pendingUnboxCurrency
    local oldBoxSelection, oldUnboxCurrency = AngelHub.setters["Box To Open"], AngelHub.setters["Pay With"]
    if oldBoxSelection then
        AngelHub.setters["Box To Open"] = function(value)
            if #unboxList == 0 and type(value) == "string" and value ~= "Waiting for boxes" then pendingBoxSelection = value; return end
            oldBoxSelection(value)
        end
    end
    if oldUnboxCurrency then
        AngelHub.setters["Pay With"] = function(value)
            if #unboxList == 0 and type(value) == "string" and value ~= "Auto" then pendingUnboxCurrency = value; return end
            oldUnboxCurrency(value)
        end
    end
    local function safeModule(module)
        if not module or not module:IsA("ModuleScript") then return nil end
        local ok, data = pcall(require, module)
        return ok and type(data) == "table" and data or nil
    end
    local function storageChild(name, child)
        local ok, storage = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
        local parent = ok and storage and storage:FindFirstChild(name)
        return parent and parent:FindFirstChild(child)
    end
    local function shopSync()
        return safeModule(storageChild("Database", "Sync"))
    end
    local function getUnboxProfile()
        return safeModule(storageChild("Modules", "ProfileData"))
    end
    local function unboxBalance(profile, currency)
        if type(profile) ~= "table" or currency == "Robux" then return nil end
        local amount = profile[currency]
        for _, section in ipairs({"Materials", "Weapons"}) do
            local item = profile[section]
            if type(amount) ~= "number" and type(item) == "table" and type(item.Owned) == "table" then amount = item.Owned[currency] end
        end
        if type(amount) == "number" and amount == amount and amount >= 0 and amount < 1000000000000 then return amount end
        return nil
    end
    local function unboxPrice(sync, id)
        local shop = sync.NewShop
        if type(shop) ~= "table" then return nil end
        local item = shop[id]
        if type(item) ~= "table" and type(shop.Weapons) == "table" then item = shop.Weapons[id] end
        if type(item) ~= "table" and type(shop.MysteryBox) == "table" then item = shop.MysteryBox[id] end
        return type(item) == "table" and type(item.Price) == "table" and item.Price or nil
    end
    local function refreshUnboxCatalog()
        if stopped or not isMM2 or unboxRefreshing then return end
        unboxRefreshing = true
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local sync = shopSync()
            if not sync then return end
            local list, ids = {}, {}
            local function add(id, data, category)
                if type(id) ~= "string" or ids[id] then return end
                local price = unboxPrice(sync, id)
                if type(price) ~= "table" then return end
                local accepted = {}
                for currency, amount in pairs(price) do
                    if type(currency) == "string" and currency ~= "Robux" and type(amount) == "number" and amount > 0 and amount < 1000000000 then
                        accepted[currency] = amount
                    end
                end
                if not next(accepted) then return end
                local name = type(data) == "table" and (type(data.Name) == "string" and data.Name
                    or (type(data.ItemData) == "table" and data.ItemData.Name)) or nil
                table.insert(list, {id = id, name = type(name) == "string" and name or id, category = category, prices = accepted})
                ids[id] = true
            end
            for _, category in ipairs({"MysteryBox", "Eggs"}) do
                if type(sync[category]) == "table" then
                    for id, data in pairs(sync[category]) do add(id, data, category) end
                end
            end
            local okStorage, storage = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
            local sharedServices = okStorage and storage and storage:FindFirstChild("SharedServices")
            local eventService = sharedServices and sharedServices:FindFirstChild("EventInfoService1")
            local direct = eventService and eventService:FindFirstChild("MysteryBoxes")
            if direct then for _, module in ipairs(direct:GetChildren()) do add(module.Name, safeModule(module), "MysteryBox") end end
            local events = eventService and eventService:FindFirstChild("EventList")
            if events then for _, event in ipairs(events:GetChildren()) do
                local module = event:FindFirstChild("MysteryBox")
                if module then add(module.Name, safeModule(module), "MysteryBox") end
            end end
            table.sort(list, function(a, b) if a.name == b.name then return a.id < b.id end return a.name < b.name end)
            local counts, labels, index, currencies = {}, {}, {}, {Auto = true}
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do counts[entry.name] = (counts[entry.name] or 0) + 1 end
            for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
                local label = counts[entry.name] > 1 and (entry.name .. " (" .. entry.id .. ")") or entry.name
                table.insert(labels, label); index[label] = entry
                for currency in pairs(entry.prices) do currencies[currency] = true end
            end
            local choices = {"Auto"}
            for currency in pairs(currencies) do if currency ~= "Auto" then table.insert(choices, currency) end end
            table.sort(choices, function(a,b) if a == "Auto" then return b ~= "Auto" elseif b == "Auto" then return false end return a < b end)
            if stopped then return end
            unboxCatalog, unboxList = index, list
            if AngelHub.cycleOptions and AngelHub.cycleOptions["Box To Open"] then
                AngelHub.cycleOptions["Box To Open"](#labels > 0 and labels or {"Waiting for boxes"})
                AngelHub.cycleOptions["Pay With"](choices)
                if #list > 0 and pendingBoxSelection then
                    local wanted = pendingBoxSelection; pendingBoxSelection = nil
                    if index[wanted] then AngelHub.setters["Box To Open"](wanted) end
                end
                if #list > 0 and pendingUnboxCurrency then
                    local wanted = pendingUnboxCurrency; pendingUnboxCurrency = nil
                    if currencies[wanted] then AngelHub.setters["Pay With"](wanted) end
                end
            end
        end)
        unboxRefreshing = false
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub box catalogue error: " .. tostring(err)) end
    end
    local function chooseUnboxCurrency(entry, profile)
        local selected = V["Pay With"]
        if selected ~= "Auto" then
            local price = entry.prices[selected]
            return price and unboxBalance(profile, selected) and selected or nil
        end
        -- Never select Gems or keys implicitly: those may have been purchased.
        local candidates = {"Coins", "Candies2025", "SnowTokens2024", "BeachBalls2025", "Candies2024", "Candies2022", "SnowTokens2023", "SnowTokens2022", "SnowTokens2020", "Eggs2025", "RareEggs2025", "Hearts2023"}
        for _, currency in ipairs(candidates) do
            local balance, price = unboxBalance(profile, currency), entry.prices[currency]
            if balance and price and balance >= price then return currency end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function stopAutoUnbox(reason)
        if enabled["Auto Unbox"] then AngelHub.setters["Auto Unbox"](false) end
        if reason then message(reason) end
    end
    local function liveUnboxEntry(sync, entry)
        local bank = sync and sync[entry.category]
        if type(bank) == "table" and type(bank[entry.id]) == "table" then return true end
        if entry.category ~= "MysteryBox" then return false end
        local ok, storage = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
        local sharedServices = ok and storage and storage:FindFirstChild("SharedServices")
        local service = sharedServices and sharedServices:FindFirstChild("EventInfoService1")
        local direct = service and service:FindFirstChild("MysteryBoxes")
        local module = direct and direct:FindFirstChild(entry.id)
        if module and module:IsA("ModuleScript") then return true end
        local events = service and service:FindFirstChild("EventList")
        if events then for _, event in ipairs(events:GetChildren()) do
            local candidate = event:FindFirstChild("MysteryBox")
            if candidate and candidate.Name == entry.id and candidate:IsA("ModuleScript") then return true end
        end end
        return false
    end
    local function tryAutoUnbox(generation)
        if stopped or not enabled["Auto Unbox"] then return end
        local label, selection = V["Box To Open"], V["Pay With"]
        local entry = unboxCatalog[label]
        if not entry then return end
        local sync = shopSync()
        if not sync or not liveUnboxEntry(sync, entry) then return end
        local livePrices = unboxPrice(sync, entry.id)
        if not livePrices then return end
        local profile = getUnboxProfile()
        if not profile then return end
        if unboxReceipt then
            local observed = unboxBalance(profile, unboxReceipt.currency)
            if not observed or observed >= unboxReceipt.balance then return end
            unboxReceipt = nil
        end
        local currency = chooseUnboxCurrency(entry, profile)
        if not currency then return end
        local balance, price = unboxBalance(profile, currency), entry.prices[currency]
        if not balance or not price or balance < price or livePrices[currency] ~= price then return end
        local shop = storageChild("Remotes", "Shop")
        local remote = shop and shop:FindFirstChild("OpenCrate")
        local complete = shop and shop:FindFirstChild("CrateComplete")
        if not remote or not remote:IsA("RemoteFunction") or not complete or not complete:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return end
        if stopped or not enabled["Auto Unbox"] or generation ~= unboxGeneration
            or V["Box To Open"] ~= label or V["Pay With"] ~= selection then return end
        -- Keep a receipt across OFF/ON until the balance actually updates.
        unboxReceipt = {currency = currency, balance = balance}
        local ok, reward = pcall(function() return remote:InvokeServer(entry.id, entry.category, currency) end)
        if not ok or type(reward) ~= "string" or reward == "" or reward == "false" or reward == "nil" then
            if not stopped then stopAutoUnbox("Auto Unbox: uncertain result; no retry until balance changes") end
            return
        end
        unboxCount += 1
        local animated = false
        if not stopped and enabled["Auto Unbox"] and generation == unboxGeneration and V["Reveal Animation"] == "On" then
            local sync = shopSync()
            if not stopped and enabled["Auto Unbox"] and generation == unboxGeneration
                and entry.category == "MysteryBox" and sync and type(sync.MysteryBox) == "table" and type(sync.MysteryBox[entry.id]) == "table"
                and type(sync.MysteryBox[entry.id].Contents) == "table" and type(sync.Weapons) == "table" and type(sync.Weapons[reward]) == "table" then
                local controller = shop:FindFirstChild("BoxController")
                if controller and controller:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                    unboxRevealUntil = os.clock() + 5
                    animated = pcall(function() controller:Fire({{MysteryBoxId = entry.id, RewardedItemId = reward}}) end)
                end
            end
        end
        if not animated then pcall(function() complete:FireServer(reward) end) end
        if not stopped then
            message("Auto Unbox: " .. tostring(reward) .. " (" .. tostring(unboxCount) .. ")")
            if enabled["Auto Unbox"] and unboxCount >= math.max(1, tonumber(V["Max Boxes Per Run"]) or 10) then stopAutoUnbox("Auto Unbox: run limit reached") end
        end
    end
    mm2UnboxStep = function()
        if not isMM2 then return end
        if os.clock() >= unboxRefreshAt then
            unboxRefreshAt = os.clock() + 15
            task.spawn(refreshUnboxCatalog)
        end
        if not enabled["Auto Unbox"] or unboxBusy or os.clock() < unboxNext or os.clock() < unboxRevealUntil then return end
        unboxNext = os.clock() + math.max(0.8, tonumber(V["Unbox Delay"]) or 1.5)
        local generation = unboxGeneration
        unboxBusy = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, err = pcall(tryAutoUnbox, generation)
            unboxBusy = false
            if not ok then
                warn("Angel Hub Auto Unbox error: " .. tostring(err))
                if not stopped then stopAutoUnbox("Auto Unbox stopped: runtime error") end
            end
        end)
    end
    function unbox.report()
        local entry = unboxCatalog[V["Box To Open"]]
        local profile = getUnboxProfile()
        if not entry then message("Auto Unbox: no priced boxes available"); return end
        local currency = chooseUnboxCurrency(entry, profile)
        if not currency then message(entry.name .. ": waiting for a known affordable balance"); return end
        message(entry.name .. ": " .. currency .. " " .. tostring(unboxBalance(profile, currency)) .. "/" .. tostring(entry.prices[currency]))
    end
    function unbox.toggle(value)
        unboxGeneration += 1
        if value then
            unboxCount, unboxNext, unboxRefreshAt = 0, 0, 0
            message("Auto Unbox spends selected currency; waits for verified balance")
        end
    end
    end
    local old=AngelHub.setters["Auto Unbox"]
    AngelHub.setters["Auto Unbox"]=function(value)
        local on=isMM2 and not stopped and value==true
        if on==V["Auto Unbox"] then return end
        old(on);unbox.toggle(on)
    end
    AngelHub.actions["Report Boxes And Coin"]=function()if not stopped and isMM2 then unbox.report()end end
    connectInput(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat,function()
        if stopped or not isMM2 then return end
        mm2UnboxStep()
    end)
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;old(false);unbox.toggle(false)end)
end

-- Auto Win MM2: safe platform and opt-in attack paths from the prior menu.
local function installMM2Win(screenGui)
    local V=AngelHub.values
    local enabled=V
    local isMM2=game.PlaceId==142823291
    local stopped=false
    local function message(text)if AngelHub.status then AngelHub.status(text) else warn("Angel Hub: "..tostring(text))end end
    local function characterParts()
        local character=localPlayer.Character
        local human=character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root=character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not human or human.Health<=0 or not root or not root:IsA("BasePart")then return character,nil,nil end
        return character,human,root
    end
    local shooter={gameplay=function()
        local ok,rs=pcall(function()return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")end)
        local remotes=ok and rs and rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        return remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Gameplay")
    end}
    local function mm2Map()return AngelHub.findMM2Map and AngelHub.findMM2Map()end
    local function spawnPart(container)
        if not container then return end
        if container:IsA("BasePart")then return container end
        return container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation",true) or container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
    end
    local function farmOnMap(root,map)
        if not root or not map or map.Parent~=workspace then return false end
        local lobby=workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("MainLobby") or workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby")
        local lobbySpawns=lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true))
        local lobbyRef=spawnPart(lobbySpawns) or (lobby and (lobby:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SpawnLocation",true) or lobby:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)))
        local spawns=map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or map:FindFirstChild("Spawns",true)
        local container=map:FindFirstChild("CoinContainer") or map:FindFirstChild("CoinAreas") or map:FindFirstChild("Coins")
        local reference=spawnPart(spawns) or spawnPart(container) or map:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true)
        return lobbyRef~=nil and reference~=nil and (root.Position-lobbyRef.Position).Magnitude>350 and (root.Position-reference.Position).Magnitude<=1500
    end
    local function knifeTargetValid(player,root,range)
        if not player or player==localPlayer or not player.Character then return end
        if not localPlayer.Neutral and localPlayer.Team and player.Team==localPlayer.Team then return end
        local character=player.Character
        local human=character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local target=character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not human or human.Health<=0 or not target or not target:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        if character:FindFirstChild("Knife") then return end
        local backpack=player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife") then return end
        if (target.Position-root.Position).Magnitude>range then return end
        return target
    end
    local autoWin = {}
    do
        local connectionList, gameplayBound = {}, nil
        local roundCounted, roundBlocked = false, false
        local safe, accumulator = nil, 0
        local participantCharacter,participantHuman,deadPending,deadAttempted=nil,nil,false,false
        local sheriffNext,grabNext=0,0
        local murderGeneration, murderBusy, murderCooldown = 0, false, {}
        local victims = {}
        local releaseSafe
        local function restoreVictim(part, snapshot)
            if victims[part] ~= snapshot then return end
            victims[part] = nil
            pcall(function() if part.CFrame == snapshot.appliedFrame then part.CFrame = snapshot.frame end end)
            pcall(function() if part.CanCollide == false then part.CanCollide = snapshot.collide end end)
            pcall(function() if part.Anchored == true then part.Anchored = snapshot.anchored end end)
        end
        local function stopMurder()
            murderGeneration += 1
            for part, snapshot in pairs(victims) do restoreVictim(part, snapshot) end
            table.clear(murderCooldown)
        end
        local reasons = {
            Innocent = {Time = true, MurdererDied = true, MurdererLeft = true, InnocentsEscaped = true},
            Hero = {Time = true, MurdererDied = true, MurdererLeft = true},
            Sheriff = {MurdererDied = true, MurdererLeft = true, SheriffWin = true},
            Murderer = {MurdererWin = true},
        }
        local counterNames = {Innocent = "Innocent Wins", Hero = "Sheriff Wins", Sheriff = "Sheriff Wins", Murderer = "Murder Wins"}
        local function disconnect()
            for _, connection in ipairs(connectionList) do connection:Disconnect() end
            table.clear(connectionList); gameplayBound = nil
        end
        local function bind()
            local gameplay = shooter.gameplay()
            if gameplay == gameplayBound and gameplay then return true end
            disconnect()
            local victory = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("VictoryScreen")
            local ended = gameplay and gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundEndFade")
            local starting = gameplay and (gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoundStart") or gameplay:FindFirstChild("RoleSelect")
                or gameplay:FindFirstChild("ShowRoleSelect") or gameplay:FindFirstChild("ShowRoleSelectNew"))
            if not victory or not victory:IsA("RemoteEvent") or not ended or not ended:IsA("RemoteEvent")
                or not starting or not starting:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return false end
            gameplayBound = gameplay
            table.insert(connectionList, victory.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(won, role, reason)
                if roundCounted then return end
                roundCounted = true
                if not enabled["Auto Win"] or won ~= true or type(role) ~= "string" or type(reason) ~= "string" then return end
                local valid, counter = reasons[role], counterNames[role]
                if not valid or not valid[reason] or not counter then return end
                local previous = tonumber(V[counter]) or 0
                if AngelHub.setters[counter] then AngelHub.setters[counter](math.max(0, math.floor(previous)) + 1) end
            end))
            table.insert(connectionList, ended.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                roundBlocked = true; stopMurder()
                if AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling then AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling("win") end
                participantCharacter,participantHuman,deadPending,deadAttempted=nil,nil,false,false
                if AngelHub.cancelWinShot then AngelHub.cancelWinShot() end
                releaseSafe()
            end))
            for _, name in ipairs({"RoundStart", "RoleSelect", "ShowRoleSelect", "ShowRoleSelectNew"}) do
                local remote = gameplay:FindFirstChild(name)
                if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
