                    table.insert(connectionList, remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(duration)
                        if name == "RoundStart" and (not tonumber(duration) or tonumber(duration) <= 0) then return end
                        stopMurder()
                        if AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling then AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling("win") end
                        participantCharacter,participantHuman,deadPending,deadAttempted=nil,nil,false,false
                        if AngelHub.cancelWinShot then AngelHub.cancelWinShot() end
                        roundCounted, roundBlocked = false, false
                    end))
                end
            end
            return true
        end
        releaseSafe = function()
            if not safe then return true end
            local parked = safe
            local character, humanoid, root = characterParts()
            local near = character == parked.character and humanoid and root
                and (root.Position - parked.position).Magnitude <= 100
            if near then
                if root.Anchored or not parked.map or parked.map.Parent ~= workspace then
                    message("Auto Win: return unavailable; platform kept")
                    return false
                end
                local ok = pcall(function()
                    character:PivotTo(parked.returnFrame)
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end)
                if not ok then message("Auto Win: return failed; platform kept"); return false end
            end
            safe = nil
            pcall(function() parked.part:Destroy() end)
            return true
        end
        local function murderStep(character, humanoid, root)
            if not enabled["Auto Win"] or not enabled["Insta Kill As Murder"] or roundBlocked or murderBusy
                or not safe or safe.character ~= character or safe.map ~= mm2Map() or safe.part.Parent ~= workspace
                or (root.Position - safe.position).Magnitude > 30 then return end
            local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            local knife = character:FindFirstChild("Knife") or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife"))
            if not knife or not knife:IsA("Tool") or knife.Enabled == false or knife:GetAttribute("Disabled") == true then return end
            local selected, selectedRoot
            local now = os.clock()
            for _, player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers()) do
                local targetRoot = knifeTargetValid(player, root, math.huge)
                if targetRoot and farmOnMap(targetRoot, safe.map) and now >= (murderCooldown[player] or 0) then
                    selected, selectedRoot = player, targetRoot; break
                end
            end
            if not selected then return end
            murderBusy = true
            local generation, parked, targetCharacter = murderGeneration, safe, selected.Character
            murderCooldown[selected] = now + 0.9
            task.spawn(function()
                local function stillValid()
                    return not stopped and enabled["Auto Win"] and enabled["Insta Kill As Murder"] and not roundBlocked
                        and generation == murderGeneration and safe == parked and parked.part.Parent == workspace and parked.map == mm2Map()
                        and localPlayer.Character == character and humanoid.Health > 0 and not root.Anchored
                        and (root.Position - parked.position).Magnitude <= 30 and selected.Character == targetCharacter
                        and knifeTargetValid(selected, root, math.huge) == selectedRoot and farmOnMap(selectedRoot, parked.map)
                end
                local ok, err = pcall(function()
                    if not stillValid() then return end
                    if knife.Parent ~= character then
                        if not backpack or knife.Parent ~= backpack then return end
                        humanoid:EquipTool(knife)
                    end
                    if not stillValid() or knife.Parent ~= character or knife.Enabled == false or knife:GetAttribute("Disabled") == true then return end
                    local handle = knife:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                    local events = knife:FindFirstChild("Events")
                    local touched = events and events:FindFirstChild("HandleTouched")
                    if not handle or not handle:IsA("BasePart") or not (touched and touched:IsA("RemoteEvent")) and type(firetouchinterest) ~= "function" then return end
                    knife:Activate()
                    if not stillValid() or knife.Parent ~= character or knife.Enabled == false or knife:GetAttribute("Disabled") == true then return end
                    if selectedRoot.Anchored then return end
                    local snapshot = {frame = selectedRoot.CFrame, anchored = selectedRoot.Anchored,
                        collide = selectedRoot.CanCollide, appliedFrame = handle.CFrame}
                    victims[selectedRoot] = snapshot
                    local sent, sendError = pcall(function()
                        selectedRoot.Anchored = true; selectedRoot.CanCollide = false; selectedRoot.CFrame = snapshot.appliedFrame
                        if touched and touched:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                            touched:FireServer(selectedRoot)
                        else
                            firetouchinterest(handle, selectedRoot, 0)
                            if stillValid() then firetouchinterest(handle, selectedRoot, 1) end
                        end
                    end)
                    restoreVictim(selectedRoot, snapshot)
                    if not sent then
                        warn("Angel Hub Murder attack uncertain: " .. tostring(sendError))
                        if generation == murderGeneration and enabled["Insta Kill As Murder"] then
                            AngelHub.setters["Insta Kill As Murder"](false); message("Auto Win Murder stopped: attack uncertain")
                        end
                    end
                end)
                murderBusy = false
                if not ok then
                    warn("Angel Hub Murder attack stopped: " .. tostring(err))
                    if generation == murderGeneration and enabled["Insta Kill As Murder"] then
                        AngelHub.setters["Insta Kill As Murder"](false); message("Auto Win Murder stopped: runtime error")
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        local function tool(name,character)
            local backpack=localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            local item=(character and character:FindFirstChild(name)) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(name))
            return item and item:IsA("Tool") and item or nil
        end
        local function rolePlayer(role,map)
            if not AngelHub.getMM2Role then return end
            for _,player in ipairs(playersService:GetPlayers())do
                local found=player~=localPlayer and AngelHub.getMM2Role(player)
                if found==role or (role=="Sheriff" and found=="Hero")then
                    local c=player.Character;local h=c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local r=c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if h and h.Health>0 and not h.Sit and r and r:IsA("BasePart") and farmOnMap(r,map) then return player end
                end
            end
        end
        local function cancelFling()
            if AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling then AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling("win")end
        end
        local function sheriffFling(character,human,root)
            if not V["Fling Sheriff And Get Gun"] or not safe or not autoWin.isSafe() then return end
            local role=AngelHub.getMM2Role and AngelHub.getMM2Role(localPlayer)
            if role~="Innocent" or tool("Knife",character) or tool("Gun",character) then return end
            local map=mm2Map()
            local drop=AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun and AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun()
            if drop then
                if os.clock()>=grabNext and AngelHub.actions["Get Gun"] then
                    grabNext=os.clock()+.3;AngelHub.actions["Get Gun"]()
                end
                return
            end
            if os.clock()<sheriffNext or os.clock()<(safe.readyAt or 0) or not AngelHub.mm2StartOwnedFling then return end
            local target=rolePlayer("Sheriff",map);if not target then return end
            local parked=safe
            local function valid()
                local targetRole=AngelHub.getMM2Role and AngelHub.getMM2Role(target)
                local currentDrop=AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun and AngelHub.findMM2DroppedGun()
                return not stopped and V["Auto Win"] and V["Fling Sheriff And Get Gun"] and not roundBlocked
                    and safe==parked and parked.part.Parent==workspace and parked.map==mm2Map()
                    and localPlayer.Character==character and human.Health>0 and not tool("Gun",character)
                    and not tool("Knife",character) and not currentDrop and (targetRole=="Sheriff" or targetRole=="Hero")
            end
            if AngelHub.mm2StartOwnedFling("win",target,valid,false) then sheriffNext=os.clock()+6 end
        end
        local function deadFling(character,human)
            if not deadPending or deadAttempted or not V["Fling Murder If Dead"] or not human or not character
                or not AngelHub.mm2StartOwnedFling then return end
            local map=mm2Map();local target=rolePlayer("Murderer",map)
            if not target then return end
            local function valid()
                return not stopped and V["Auto Win"] and V["Fling Murder If Dead"] and not roundBlocked
                    and deadPending and localPlayer.Character==character and human.Health>0 and mm2Map()==map
                    and AngelHub.getMM2Role and AngelHub.getMM2Role(target)=="Murderer"
            end
            if AngelHub.mm2StartOwnedFling("win",target,valid,true) then deadAttempted=true end
        end
        function autoWin.murderToggle() stopMurder() end
        function autoWin.canStart()
            if safe and not releaseSafe() then return false end
            return bind()
        end
        function autoWin.toggle(value)
            stopMurder();cancelFling()
            if not value then
                participantCharacter,participantHuman,deadPending,deadAttempted=nil,nil,false,false
                releaseSafe(); return
            end
            accumulator = 0;sheriffNext=0;grabNext=0
            message("Auto Win: experimental; attacks and fling depend on server physics")
        end
        function autoWin.step(dt, character, humanoid, root)
            if not enabled["Auto Win"] then return end
            if AngelHub.mm2FlingOwner=="win" then return end
            if participantHuman and participantHuman.Health<=0 and not deadAttempted then deadPending=true end
            accumulator += dt
            if accumulator < 0.1 then return end
            accumulator = 0
            if safe then
                if character ~= safe.character or not humanoid or not root
                    or (root.Position - safe.position).Magnitude > 100 then
                    releaseSafe()
                end
                if safe then
                    if not roundBlocked and character and humanoid and root then
                        murderStep(character, humanoid, root)
                        sheriffFling(character,humanoid,root)
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
            if roundBlocked or not character or not humanoid or not root or root.Anchored then return end
            local map = mm2Map()
            if deadPending then deadFling(character,humanoid);return end
            if not farmOnMap(root, map) then return end
            local destroyY = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
            if type(destroyY) ~= "number" or destroyY ~= destroyY or math.abs(destroyY) > 1000000 then destroyY = -500 end
            if root.Position.Y <= destroyY + 100 then return end
            local original = root.CFrame
            if humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
                local spawns = map:FindFirstChild("Spawns") or map:FindFirstChild("Spawns", true)
                local spawn = spawnPart(spawns)
                if not spawn then return end
                original = spawn.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
            end
            local destination = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 120, 0)
            local platform
            local ok = pcall(function()
                platform = Instance.new("Part")
                platform.Name = "AngelHub_AutoWinSafe"
                platform.Anchored = true; platform.CanCollide = true; platform.CanQuery = false; platform.CanTouch = false
                platform.CastShadow = false; platform.Size = Vector3.new(64, 4, 64)
                platform.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; platform.Transparency = 0.15
                platform.CFrame = CFrame.new(destination); platform.Parent = workspace
            end)
            if not ok or not platform or not platform.Parent then
                if platform then pcall(function() platform:Destroy() end) end
                AngelHub.setters["Auto Win"](false); message("Auto Win stopped: safe platform unavailable"); return
            end
            safe = {part = platform, position = destination, character = character, map = map, returnFrame = original,readyAt=os.clock()+2}
            participantCharacter,participantHuman=character,humanoid
            local moved = pcall(function() character:PivotTo(CFrame.new(destination + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))) end)
            if not moved then
                releaseSafe()
                AngelHub.setters["Auto Win"](false); message("Auto Win stopped: platform move uncertain"); return
            end
            pcall(function()
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end)
        end
        function autoWin.release() return releaseSafe() end
        function autoWin.returnSafe(character,home)
            return safe~=nil and safe.character==character and safe.part.Parent==workspace and home~=nil
                and (home.Position-safe.position).Magnitude<=50
        end
        function autoWin.isSafe()
            local character,human,root=characterParts()
            return not roundBlocked and safe~=nil and character==safe.character and human~=nil and root~=nil
                and safe.map==mm2Map() and safe.part.Parent==workspace and (root.Position-safe.position).Magnitude<=30
        end
        function autoWin.cleanup()
            stopMurder();cancelFling()
            disconnect()
            releaseSafe()
        end
    end
    AngelHub.winSafe=autoWin.isSafe;AngelHub.winReturnSafe=autoWin.returnSafe
    local old=AngelHub.setters["Auto Win"]
    local function conflict()
        return V["Auto Farm"] or V["Enable Speed"] or V["Noclip"] or V["Air Walk"] or V["Air Swim"] or V["Enable Fly"]
            or V["FreeCam"] or V["Walk Fling"] or V["Auto Fling Selected Target"] or V["Auto Vote"] or V["Auto Knife Throw"]
            or (AngelHub.mm2FlingBusy and AngelHub.mm2FlingOwner~="win")
    end
    AngelHub.setters["Auto Win"]=function(value)
        local on=isMM2 and not stopped and value==true
        if on and V["Auto Win"] then return end
        if on and (conflict() or not autoWin.canStart()) then old(false);message("Auto Win: señales o retorno no disponibles, o movimiento incompatible");return false end
        if AngelHub.cancelWinShot then AngelHub.cancelWinShot()end
        old(on)
        if on and not V["Auto Win"] then autoWin.toggle(false);return false end
        autoWin.toggle(on)
        if on and AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles()
        elseif AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles then AngelHub.releaseMM2Roles()end
    end
    local murderSetter=AngelHub.setters["Insta Kill As Murder"]
    AngelHub.setters["Insta Kill As Murder"]=function(value)murderSetter(isMM2 and not stopped and value==true);autoWin.murderToggle()end
    local sheriffSetter=AngelHub.setters["Insta Kill As Sheriff"]
    AngelHub.setters["Insta Kill As Sheriff"]=function(value)
        if AngelHub.cancelWinShot then AngelHub.cancelWinShot()end
        sheriffSetter(isMM2 and not stopped and value==true)
    end
    for _,name in ipairs({"Fling Sheriff And Get Gun","Fling Murder If Dead"})do
        local setter=AngelHub.setters[name]
        AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value)
            setter(isMM2 and not stopped and value==true)
            if not value and AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling then AngelHub.mm2CancelOwnedFling("win")end
        end
    end
    for _,name in ipairs({"Auto Farm","Enable Speed","Noclip","Enable Fly","Auto Vote","Auto Fling Selected Target","Auto Knife Throw"})do
        local setter=AngelHub.setters[name]
        if setter then AngelHub.setters[name]=function(value)
            if value==true and V["Auto Win"] then setter(false);return false end
            if value==true and not autoWin.release() then return false end
            return setter(value)
        end end
    end
    connectInput(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat,function(dt)
        if stopped or not V["Auto Win"] then return end
        if conflict() then AngelHub.setters["Auto Win"](false);return end
        if AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles then AngelHub.refreshMM2Roles()end
        local character,human,root=characterParts()
        local ok,err=pcall(autoWin.step,dt,character,human,root)
        if not ok then warn("Angel Hub Auto Win: "..tostring(err));AngelHub.setters["Auto Win"](false);return end
        if AngelHub.mm2FlingOwner~="win" and V["Insta Kill As Sheriff"] and AngelHub.winShoot then
            local shotOK,shotError=pcall(AngelHub.winShoot)
            if not shotOK then warn("Angel Hub Auto Win Sheriff: "..tostring(shotError));AngelHub.setters["Insta Kill As Sheriff"](false)end
        end
    end)
    screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()stopped=true;old(false);autoWin.cleanup();AngelHub.winSafe=nil;AngelHub.winReturnSafe=nil end)
end

local function initializeScript()
	if typeof(shared) == "table" and shared.AngelHubGui then
		pcall(function()
			shared.AngelHubGui:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	local guiParent = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

	if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
		local success, hiddenParent = pcall(gethui)
		if success and hiddenParent then
			guiParent = hiddenParent
		end
	end

	local screenGui = create("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "AngelHubGui",
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
	}, guiParent)

	if typeof(shared) == "table" then
		shared.AngelHubGui = screenGui
		shared.AngelHub = AngelHub
	end

	screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
		for _, connection in AngelHub.connections do
			connection:Disconnect()
		end

		table.clear(AngelHub.connections)
	end)

	local window = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WINDOW_WIDTH, WINDOW_HEIGHT),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -WINDOW_WIDTH / 2, 0.5, -WINDOW_HEIGHT / 2),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
	}, screenGui)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, window)
	create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(BACKGROUND_TOP, BACKGROUND_BOTTOM), Rotation = 90 }, window)

	local windowStroke = addStroke(window, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.2, 1.5)
	addGradient(windowStroke, 45)

	local windowScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.85 }, window)
	tween(windowScale, POP_TWEEN, { Scale = 1 })

	local sheen = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, window)
	create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.965, 1), Rotation = 90 }, sheen)

	local titleBar = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TITLE_HEIGHT), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, window)
	local titleGradient = addGradient(titleBar, 0)
	titleGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.8, 0.92)

	local titleLine = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, titleBar)

	addGradient(titleLine, 0)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -260, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		RichText = true,
		Text = "<b>" .. TITLE_TEXT .. "</b>",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 14,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
	}, titleBar)

	local searchBox = create("TextBox", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 22),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -200, 0.5, -11),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		PlaceholderText = "Search",
		PlaceholderColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		Text = "",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
	}, titleBar)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, searchBox)
	local searchStroke = addStroke(searchBox, ACCENT_A, 1)

	searchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
		tween(searchStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 0 })
	end)

	searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		tween(searchStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 1 })
	end)

	local minimizeButton = createTitleButton(titleBar, "-", -60, ACCENT_B)
	local closeButton = createTitleButton(titleBar, "X", -32, Color3.fromRGB(230, 60, 70))

	makeDraggable(titleBar, window)

	local minimized = false
	local windowWidth = WINDOW_WIDTH
	local windowHeight = WINDOW_HEIGHT

	minimizeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		minimized = not minimized
		AngelHub.resizeHandle.Visible = not minimized
		tween(window, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(windowWidth, minimized and TITLE_HEIGHT or windowHeight) })
	end)

	closeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		tween(windowScale, FAST_TWEEN, { Scale = 0 }).Completed:Connect(function()
			screenGui:Destroy()
		end)
	end)

	local tabBar = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TAB_HEIGHT), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, TITLE_HEIGHT), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, window)

	local indicator = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1 / #TAB_NAMES, -12, 1, -6),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 3),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.9,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, tabBar)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, indicator)
	addStroke(indicator, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.8)

	local underline = create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 2),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, indicator)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, underline)
	addGradient(underline, 0)
	AngelHub.indicator = indicator

	local contentTop = TITLE_HEIGHT + TAB_HEIGHT + 8
	local content = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -(contentTop + 10)), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, contentTop), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, window)

	local resizeHandle = create("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "",
		ZIndex = 10,
	}, window)

	AngelHub.resizeHandle = resizeHandle

	for gripIndex = 1, 3 do
		create("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(gripIndex * 4, 1),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -3, 1, -3 - (3 - gripIndex) * 4 - 2),
			BackgroundColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
		}, resizeHandle)
	end

	local resizing = false
	local resizeStart, startWidth, startHeight

	resizeHandle.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		for _, grip in resizeHandle:GetChildren() do
			tween(grip, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT_A })
		end
	end)

	resizeHandle.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if resizing then
			return
		end

		for _, grip in resizeHandle:GetChildren() do
			tween(grip, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
		end
	end)

	resizeHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) then
			return
		end

		resizing = true
		resizeStart = input.Position
		startWidth, startHeight = windowWidth, windowHeight
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if not resizing or not isMove(input) then
			return
		end

		local delta = input.Position - resizeStart
		windowWidth = math.clamp(startWidth + delta.X, MIN_WIDTH, MAX_WIDTH)
		windowHeight = math.clamp(startHeight + delta.Y, MIN_HEIGHT, MAX_HEIGHT)
		window.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(windowWidth, windowHeight)
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
		if not resizing or not isPress(input) then
			return
		end

		resizing = false
		for _, grip in resizeHandle:GetChildren() do
			tween(grip, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
		end
	end)

	for index, name in TAB_NAMES do
		local button = create("TextButton", {
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 / #TAB_NAMES, 1),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale((index - 1) / #TAB_NAMES, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = name,
			TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
			TextSize = 13,
			Font = BOLD_FONT,
			AutoButtonColor = false,
		}, tabBar)

		local page = create("CanvasGroup", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, content)

		button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if AngelHub.selected ~= name then
				tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT_COLOR })
			end
		end)

		button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			if AngelHub.selected ~= name then
				tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
			end
		end)

		button.Activated:Connect(function()
			switchTab(name)
		end)

		AngelHub.pages[name] = page
		AngelHub.tabButtons[name] = button
		AngelHub.tabIndex[name] = index
	end

	-- Main tab.
	local mainLeft = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Main, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local mainRight = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Main, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	-- Target Player only shows when Aimbot Target is Custom.

		if not targetPlayerHolder then
			return
		end

		local hidden = AngelHub.values["Aimbot Target"] ~= "Custom"
		targetPlayerHolder:SetAttribute("GroupHidden", hidden)
		targetPlayerHolder.Visible = not hidden
	end

	addSection(mainLeft, "Gun Aimbot")
	addModule(mainLeft, "Gun Aimbot", nil, false)
	addCycle(mainLeft, "Aim Method", { "Normal", "Instakill" })

	addSection(mainLeft, "Auto Shoot")

	local proximityHolder = addSlider(mainLeft, "Shoot Proximity Aura", 5, 150, 30, 0)
	local proximitySetter = AngelHub.setters["Shoot Proximity"]
	AngelHub.setters["Shoot Proximity"] = function(value)
		proximitySetter(value)
		local hidden = AngelHub.values["Shoot Proximity"] ~= true

	end
	AngelHub.setters["Shoot Proximity"](false)

	addSection(mainLeft, "Gun Aura")
	addModule(mainLeft, "Gun Aura", nil, false)
	addSlider(mainLeft, "Gun Aura Studs", 5, 250, 50, 0)
	addToggle(mainLeft, "Gun Aura Show Hitbox", nil, false)

	addSection(mainRight, "Knife Stab")

	addModule(mainRight, "Auto Fling Selected Target", nil, false)
	addSlider(mainRight, "Fling Retries", 0, 3, 1, 0)
    local flingStatusHolder=createCard("Frame",mainRight,"Fling Status",40)
    local flingStatusLabel=create("TextLabel",{Size=UDim2.new(1,-16,1,0),Position=UDim2.fromOffset(8,0),
        BackgroundTransparency=1,Text="Selecciona un jugador",TextColor3=OFF_TEXT_COLOR,TextSize=12,
        Font=TEXT_FONT,TextWrapped=true,TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left},flingStatusHolder)
    AngelHub.setFlingStatus=function(text)flingStatusLabel.Text=tostring(text)end
    table.insert(AngelHub.searchables,flingStatusHolder)

	addSection(mainRight, "Anti Fling")
	addModule(mainRight, "Anti Fling", nil, false)
	addToggle(mainRight, "Player Shield", nil, true)
	addToggle(mainRight, "Impact Shield", nil, true)

	-- Extra tab.
	local extraLeft = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Extra, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local extraRight = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Extra, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	addSection(extraLeft, "Auto Farm")

	addCounter(extraLeft, "Farm Time", 0)
	addModule(extraLeft, "Auto Farm", nil, false)
	addSlider(extraLeft, "Farm Speed", 5, 60, 16, 0)

	beginGroup(extraLeft, "Farm Settings", false)

	addCycle(extraRight, "Gravity Method", { "Workspace", "Force" })

	addButton(extraRight, "Reset Gravity", function() if AngelHub.resetGravity then AngelHub.resetGravity() end end)

	addSection(extraRight, "MM2 Teleport")
	addButton(extraRight, "Teleport To Map")
	addButton(extraRight, "Teleport To Lobby")
	addText(extraRight, "Teleport Player Name", "Player name")
	addButton(extraRight, "Teleport To Player")

	addKeybind(extraRight, "Get Gun Key", "G")

	addModule(extraRight, "Walk Fling", nil, false)

	-- Visuals tab.
	local visualsLeft = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Visuals, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local visualsRight = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Visuals, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	addSection(visualsLeft, "Player ESP")

	addCycle(visualsLeft, "ESP Boxes", { "Corner", "Full", "Off" })

	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Filled Box", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Skeleton", nil, false)
	addCycle(visualsLeft, "Tracers", { "Off", "Bottom", "Centre", "Cursor" })
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "ESP Visibility Check", nil, false)

	addSection(visualsLeft, "Chams")
	addModule(visualsLeft, "Chams", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsLeft, "Chams Fill Transparency", 0, 1, 0.5, 2)
	addSlider(visualsLeft, "Chams Outline Transparency", 0, 1, 0, 2)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Chams Through Walls", nil, true)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Chams Show Perk", nil, false)

	addSection(visualsLeft, "Nametags")
	addModule(visualsLeft, "Nametags", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Display Name", nil, true)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Account Name", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Nametag Distance", nil, true)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Show Role", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsLeft, "Nametag Text Size", 10, 24, 14, 0)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Nametag Through Walls", nil, false)

	addSection(visualsLeft, "HP")
	addModule(visualsLeft, "HP Players", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "HP Health Text", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "HP Through Walls", nil, true)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Self HP", nil, false)
	addCycle(visualsLeft, "Self HP Position", { "Center", "Bottom" })
	addSlider(visualsLeft, "Self HP Scale", 0.8, 1.6, 1, 2)

	addSection(visualsLeft, "Role Colours")
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Role Colours", nil, true)
	addColor(visualsLeft, "Murderer Colour", Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70))
	addColor(visualsLeft, "Sheriff Colour", Color3.fromRGB(70, 160, 255))
	addColor(visualsLeft, "Hero Colour", Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 70))
	addColor(visualsLeft, "Innocent Colour", Color3.fromRGB(70, 255, 120))
	addColor(visualsLeft, "Dead Colour", Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 124))
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Murderer ESP", nil, true)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Sheriff ESP", nil, true)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Hero ESP", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Innocent ESP", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Dead ESP", nil, false)

	addSection(visualsLeft, "Coin ESP")
	addModule(visualsLeft, "Coin ESP", nil, false)
	addCycle(visualsLeft, "Coin In Use", { "Auto (Server)", "Coin", "Candy", "SnowToken", "BeachBall", "Egg", "Heart", "All" })
	addColor(visualsLeft, "Coin Colour", Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 70))
	addSlider(visualsLeft, "Coin Transparency", 0, 1, 0, 2)

	addSection(visualsLeft, "Improve FPS")
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Remove Textures", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Disable Particles", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Disable Shadows", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Simple Materials", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Lowest Render Quality", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Low Detail Meshes", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Hide Distant Scenery", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Simplify Terrain", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Disable Post Effects", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Simple Lighting", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Disable Local Lights", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Simplify Game UI", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Hide World UI", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Hide Game UI", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsLeft, "Hide Roblox Panels", nil, false)

	-- MM2 only item ESP.
	addSection(visualsRight, "Item ESP")
	addModule(visualsRight, "Gun ESP", nil, true)
	addModule(visualsRight, "Trap ESP", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsRight, "Item Show Distance", nil, true)
	addToggle(visualsRight, "Item Outline", nil, true)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "Item Text Size", 8, 22, 13, 0)

	addSection(visualsRight, "X-Ray")
	addModule(visualsRight, "X-Ray", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "XRay Transparency", 0, 1, 0.5, 2)

	addSection(visualsRight, "Fullbright")
	addModule(visualsRight, "Fullbright", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "Fullbright Brightness", 0, 2, 1, 2)
	addModule(visualsRight, "Time Changer", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "Time Value", 0, 24, 14, 1)
	addModule(visualsRight, "Fast Time", nil, false)

	addSection(visualsRight, "FOV")
	addModule(visualsRight, "Custom FOV", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "FOV Value", 20, 120, 70, 0)

	addSection(visualsRight, "Zoom")
	addModule(visualsRight, "Custom Zoom", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "Zoom Max Distance", 25, 2000, 400, 0)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "Zoom Min Distance", 0.5, 50, 0.5, 1)
	addToggle(visualsRight, "Unlock Forced First Person", nil, false)
	addToggle(visualsRight, "Zoom Through Walls", nil, false)

	addSection(visualsRight, "FreeCam")
	addModule(visualsRight, "FreeCam", nil, false)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "FreeCam Speed", 1, 250, 50, 0)
	addSlider(visualsRight, "FreeCam Sensitivity", 0.05, 0.6, 0.2, 2)
	addToggle(visualsRight, "FreeCam Spectate", nil, false)
	addList(visualsRight, "Spectate Player", { "Player1", "Player2" }, 60)


	-- Config tab.
	local configLeft = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Config, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local configRight = createColumn(AngelHub.pages.Config, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	local statusLabel = create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 18),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -20),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "",
		TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ZIndex = 5,
	}, AngelHub.pages.Config)

	local function setStatus(message)
		statusLabel.Text = message
		statusLabel.TextTransparency = 0

		task.delay(3, function()
			if statusLabel.Text == message then
				tween(statusLabel, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { TextTransparency = 1 })
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Config files use Config Name; blank falls back to the default file.
	local function getConfigPath()
		local name = string.gsub(tostring(AngelHub.values["Config Name"] or ""), "[^%w_%- ]", "")
		if name == "" then
			return CONFIG_FILE
		end

		return CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
	end

	-- Color3 is not JSON friendly, so it is stored as a tagged table.
	local function encodeValues()
		local data = {}

		for name, value in AngelHub.values do
			if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
				data[name] = {
					R = math.floor(value.R * 255 + 0.5),
					G = math.floor(value.G * 255 + 0.5),
					B = math.floor(value.B * 255 + 0.5),
					__color = true,
				}
			else
				data[name] = value
			end
		end

		return httpService:JSONEncode(data)
	end

	local function writeConfig(path)
		if not writefile then
			return false, "Saving is not supported here."
		end

		return pcall(function()
			if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then
				makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER)
			end

			writefile(path, encodeValues())
		end)
	end

	local function readConfig(path)
		if not (readfile and isfile and isfile(path)) then
			return nil, "No saved settings found."
		end

		local ok, data = pcall(function()
			return httpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path))
		end)

		if not ok or typeof(data) ~= "table" then
			return nil, "Settings file is corrupted."
		end

		return data
	end

	local function applyConfig(data)
        if data["WallCheck"] == nil then data["WallCheck"] = data["Aimbot WallCheck"] end
        if data["Macro"] == nil then data["Macro"] = data["Aimbot Macro"] end
        local legacyAim = {Bullet="Normal", ["Normal(Bullet)"]="Normal", Experimental="Normal",
            CFrame="Instakill", ["Silent AIM"]="Instakill", ["Silent(CFrame)"]="Instakill", ["Silent(Cframe)"]="Instakill"}
        data["Aim Method"] = legacyAim[data["Aim Method"]] or data["Aim Method"]
        data["Shoot Mode"] = legacyAim[data["Shoot Mode"]] or data["Shoot Mode"]
		for name, value in data do
			local setter = AngelHub.setters[name]
			if not setter then
				continue
			end

			if typeof(value) == "table" and value.__color then
				value = Color3.fromRGB(value.R, value.G, value.B)
			end

			pcall(setter, value)
		end
	end

	local function saveSettings()
		local ok, result = writeConfig(getConfigPath())
		setStatus(ok and "Settings saved." or ("Save failed: " .. tostring(result)))
	end

	local function loadSettings()
		local data, message = readConfig(getConfigPath())
		if not data then
			return setStatus(message)
		end

		applyConfig(data)
		setStatus("Settings loaded.")
	end

	local function resetSettings()
		for name, value in AngelHub.defaults do
			AngelHub.setters[name](value)
		end

		setStatus("Settings reset.")
	end

	addSection(configLeft, "Menu")
	addCycle(configLeft, "Menu Keybind", { "RightShift", "Insert", "RightControl", "Delete", "End" })

	addSection(configLeft, "Configs")
	addText(configLeft, "Config Name", "my config")
	addButton(configLeft, "Create", saveSettings)
	addButton(configLeft, "Load", loadSettings)
	addButton(configLeft, "Save", saveSettings)
	addToggle(configLeft, "Autoload", nil, false)

	addSection(configLeft, "Utility")
	addModule(configLeft, "Anti AFK", nil, true)
	addButton(configLeft, "Rejoin Server", function()
		teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, localPlayer)
	end)

	addSection(configRight, "Unload")
	addButton(configRight, "Reset Settings", resetSettings)
	addButton(configRight, "Unload", function()
		tween(windowScale, FAST_TWEEN, { Scale = 0 }).Completed:Connect(function()
			screenGui:Destroy()
		end)
	end)

	-- Keep MM2 sections out of view in other experiences, including searches.
	if game.PlaceId ~= 142823291 then
		local mm2Boxes = {
			["Gun Aimbot Box"]=true, ["Auto Shoot Box"]=true, ["Gun Aura Box"]=true,
			["Knife Stab Box"]=true, ["Knife Throw Box"]=true, ["Fling Box"]=true,
			["Auto Farm Box"]=true, ["Auto Win Box"]=true, ["Auto Vote Box"]=true,
			["Auto Unbox Box"]=true, ["Blurt Roles Box"]=true, ["Coins Aura Box"]=true,
			["MM2 Teleport Box"]=true, ["MM2 Sprint Tail Box"]=true, ["Role Colours Box"]=true, ["Coin ESP Box"]=true,
			["Item ESP Box"]=true,
		}
		for _, box in AngelHub.boxes do
			if mm2Boxes[box.Name] then box:SetAttribute("MM2Hidden", true); box.Visible=false end
		end
	end

    AngelHub.status=setStatus
	installGunFeatures(screenGui)
	installMovementFeatures(screenGui)
	installAntiFlingFeatures(screenGui)
	installPlayerVisuals(screenGui)
	installChams(screenGui)
	installCoinESP(screenGui)
	installBasicUtilities(screenGui)
	installAutoVote(screenGui)
	installItemESP(screenGui)
	installBasicActions(screenGui)
	installPlayerLists(screenGui)
	installMM2Fling(screenGui)
	installMM2Farm(screenGui)
	installMM2Unbox(screenGui)
	installMM2Win(screenGui)

	-- Apply the autosaved config on startup if Autoload was enabled.
	local savedData = readConfig(CONFIG_FILE)
	if savedData and savedData.Autoload == true then
        for _,name in ipairs({"Auto Farm","Auto Win","Auto Unbox","Auto Fling Selected Target"}) do savedData[name]=false end
		applyConfig(savedData)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while screenGui.Parent do
			if AngelHub.values["Autoload"] then
				writeConfig(CONFIG_FILE)
			end

			task.wait(5)
		end
	end)

	searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local query = string.lower(searchBox.Text)

		for _, element in AngelHub.searchables do
			element.Visible = (query == "" or string.find(string.lower(element.Name), query, 1, true) ~= nil) and not element:GetAttribute("GroupHidden")
		end

		for _, section in AngelHub.sections do
			section.Visible = query == ""
		end

		for _, box in AngelHub.boxes do
			local anyVisible = query == ""

			for _, child in box:GetChildren() do
				if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible and not table.find(AngelHub.sections, child) then
					anyVisible = true
					break
				end
			end

			box.Visible = anyVisible and not box:GetAttribute("MM2Hidden")
		end
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gameProcessed)
		if AngelHub.listening then
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return AngelHub.listening(input.KeyCode) end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then
                return AngelHub.listening(input.UserInputType)
            end
            return
		end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
        if userInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
		if input.KeyCode.Name == AngelHub.values["Menu Keybind"] then
			window.Visible = not window.Visible
			return
		end
        if gameProcessed then return end

		for featureName, keyCode in AngelHub.keybinds do
			if keyCode == input.KeyCode then
				AngelHub.setters[featureName](not AngelHub.values[featureName])
			end
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while screenGui.Parent do
			local visible = AngelHub.values["Keybind Hints"] ~= false

			for _, keybindButton in AngelHub.keyLabels do
				keybindButton.Visible = visible
			end

			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	switchTab("Main")
end

local function onInitializeError(error)
	warn("Failed to initialize.")
	warn(error)
	warn(debug.traceback())
end

xpcall(initializeScript, onInitializeError)
